### [1] [Gaussian Sculpting: End-to-End Controllable Surface Reconstruction via Field Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10602)
*Ke Jiaxin,Juncheng Liu,Yi Wang,Zhouhui Lian,Bin Liu,Shengfa Wang,Xiangjia He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaussian Sculpting，一个完全可微的端到端框架，将3D高斯基元锚定在可演化的隐式表面（SDF）上，通过双层训练策略（外层优化SDF几何、内层在固定几何下更新高斯基元）实现高精度表面重建，并引入八叉树式多分辨率细分方案在保持细节的同时降低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然在实时新视角合成上表现出色，但在有限视角下难以恢复精确表面，且高斯基元固有的不规则性导致几何误差难以手动校正。现有方法仅在后期处理中从高斯基元提取表面，缺乏端到端的几何引导。

Method: 提出双层训练策略（bi-level training）：外层循环优化SDF几何，内层循环在固定几何下更新高斯基元，实现稳定的梯度隔离；将高斯基元锚定在演化中的可微表面上以引导SDF优化；对高斯参数施加约束以确保与底层表面一致性；引入基于八叉树划分的多分辨率细分方案以保留细节并减少内存占用。

Result: 在物体级场景上的实验表明，该方法有效去除冗余表面，恢复因有限视角缺失的结构，甚至在较低分辨率下也能实现强健的重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过将高斯基元与隐式SDF表面深度耦合并采用双层优化策略，Gaussian Sculpting在保持3DGS实时渲染优势的同时显著提升了表面重建的几何精度，为3DGS框架下的高质量表面重建提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently enabled real-time novel view synthesis with impressive quality. However, it struggles to recover accurate surfaces under limited viewpoints and due to the inherent irregularity of Gaussian primitives. The resulting geometric errors are notoriously difficult to correct manually. To address these issues, we propose Gaussian Sculpting, a fully differentiable end-to-end framework for high-quality surface reconstruction. Our key insight is to anchor Gaussians onto an evolving differentiable surface, allowing them to guide signed distance field (SDF) optimization instead of extracting the surface only during post-processing. To enable stable gradient isolation during joint optimization, we design a bi-level training strategy in which the outer loop optimizes the geometry represented by the SDF, while the inner loop updates the Gaussians with the geometry fixed. We further impose constraints on Gaussian parameters to ensure consistency with the underlying surface, thereby improving both geometric and appearance fidelity during optimization. In addition, we introduce a multi-resolution subdivision scheme based on octree-like partitioning to preserve fine details while reducing memory consumption. Experiments on object-level scenes demonstrate that our method effectively removes redundant surfaces, recovers missing structures caused by limited viewpoints, and achieves strong reconstruction quality even at relatively low resolutions.

</details>


### [2] [The GENEA Challenge 2026: A Large-Scale Disentangled Evaluation of Speech-Driven Gesture Generation on the Seamless Interaction Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10839)
*Rajmund Nagy,Silvia Arellano García,Hendric Voss,Mihail Tsakov,Taras Kucherenko,Youngwoo Yoon,Gustav Eje Henter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了第四届GENEA Challenge的评估结果，对5个语音驱动手势生成系统进行了大规模人工评估，共开展4项用户研究、收集23,000余票。结果显示：数据集的运动质量和语音对齐均显著优于所有参赛系统；在对话响应性和语义表达两个新任务上，参赛系统均未能显著超越随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音驱动的手势生成是对话智能体的关键能力，但已有评估方法往往混淆运动质量和语音对齐两个维度；同时现有挑战多关注单方手势生成，缺乏对语义表达和对话响应性的系统评估。本文旨在通过解耦评估和新增任务，更全面地衡量当前技术水平的真实差距。

Method: 采用解耦评估方法论分别评估运动质量和语音对齐，避免两者相互干扰；执行双人（dyadic）不匹配研究以隔离听和回应对话者带来的效果；新增语义手势生成任务，引入基于Grounded Gestures子集的文本不匹配评估方法。共开展四项大规模用户研究，收集869名测试者超过23,000票。

Result: 运动现实主义研究中，数据集过滤片段的运动质量大幅高于所有参赛作品（68-95%成对胜率）；语音对齐研究中，动捕片段达到62%对齐分数的概念上限，最佳提交仅32%，其余仅略高于0%；双人研究中动捕设定65%适当性分数上限，但所有提交均未显著高于随机水平；语义不匹配评估中数据集手势具有高度表现力（79%正确识别），但最佳提交仅获8%适当性分数。

Conclusion: 当前语音驱动手势生成系统在运动质量和语音对齐上仍与真实动捕数据存在显著差距，且在对话响应性和语义表达两个关键维度上几乎不具备实际能力。数据集投票和输出将公开以促进可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: This preprint presents the results of the fourth GENEA Challenge, a large-scale human evaluation of five speech-driven gesture-generation systems trained by participating teams on the Seamless Interaction dataset of dyadic conversations. As in the 2023 GENEA Challenge, we used a disentangled evaluation methodology to assess motion quality and speech alignment without confounding between the two, and performed a dyadic mismatching study to isolate the effect of listening and reacting to the interlocutor. We additionally introduce a new semantic gesture-generation task and a text-mismatching evaluation methodology using the Grounded Gestures subset of the data. In total, we ran four large-scale user studies, collecting over 23,000 votes from 869 test-takers.
  In the motion-realism study, the dataset's filtered segments had substantially higher motion quality than all challenge submissions (68-95% pairwise winrate). In the speech-alignment study, the motion-capture segments provided a conceptual ceiling at 62% alignment score, with the top submission significantly behind at 32% and the rest only slightly above the 0% expected of an input-independent system. In the dyadic study, motion capture again set the ceiling at 65% appropriateness score, but no submission scored substantially above chance, indicating that the systems could not yet respond to the interlocutor. Finally, the semantic mismatching evaluation found highly expressive gestures in the dataset (test-takers identified the matching transcript 79% of the time), yet almost all submissions failed to generate semantically expressive motion, with the best achieving only an 8% appropriateness score.
  The collected votes and outputs will be made publicly available at https://genea-workshop.github.io/2026/challenge/ to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [3] [LEGO: Leveled Language Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10057)
*Yuning Peng,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu,Yipeng Lu,Zhen Dong,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEGO是一种面向开放词汇3D场景理解的新方法，通过将多视角2D分割粒度的不一致性自适应性重分级为统一的3D一致层次结构，结合CLIP嵌入恢复跨层级的开放词汇语义逻辑，并引入空间关系构建层级化语言场景图，赋能大语言模型进行复杂空间推理和视觉定位，在多个3D分割基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型（如SAM）虽然能在2D中识别多粒度结构，但其分割结果严格受视角限制、缺乏跨视角一致性，无法直接用于3D场景的层级化理解；同时现有开放词汇3D分割方法多聚焦于基本概念识别，忽略了场景中固有的语义层级关系（如"花盆->花束->花蕾->花瓣"这种谱系关系）。

Method: LEGO的核心方法包括三个关键步骤：(1) 自适应性将多视角SAM产生的波动性2D分割粒度重新分级为统一的、3D一致的层级结构；(2) 利用该层级结构提供精确监督，实现结构连贯的多层级3D场景分割；(3) 用CLIP嵌入对分割结果进行语义锚定以恢复开放词汇语义逻辑，并融入空间关系将分割提升为层级化语言场景图，赋能大语言模型进行复杂空间推理和精确视觉定位。

Result: 实验结果显示LEGO在可提示（promptable）和开放词汇3D分割基准上均取得了新的SOTA性能，展现了先进的层级化场景分解能力和上下文感知的空间推理能力。

Conclusion: LEGO通过将2D基础模型的多粒度分割能力在3D空间中统一为一致层级结构，并结合CLIP和大语言模型，有效弥合了开放词汇语义理解与细粒度3D场景结构之间的鸿沟，为复杂3D场景理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce LEGO for advanced open-vocabulary scene understanding. Beyond basic concept recognition, its core innovation lies in capturing the intrinsic semantic hierarchies within the scene, such as the "flowerpot -> bouquet -> bud -> petal" lineage. While foundation models like SAM can identify multi-granular structures in 2D, their partitions are strictly perspective-bound and lack cross-view consensus. LEGO self-adaptively re-grades volatile multi-view SAM granularities into a unified, 3D-consistent hierarchy. This provides precise supervision for the structurally coherent, multi-level segmentation of 3D scenes. By grounding these segments with CLIP embeddings, LEGO recovers open-vocabulary semantic logic across hierarchical levels. Furthermore, by incorporating spatial relationships, we elevate these segments into level-wise language scene graphs, effectively empowering Large Language Models to perform complex, context-aware spatial reasoning and precise visual grounding. Experimental results demonstrate that LEGO establishes new state-of-the-art performance across both promptable and open-vocabulary 3D segmentation benchmarks, exhibiting advanced hierarchical scene decomposition and context-aware spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [4] [Signpost Watermarking: Joint Optimization for Visual Watermark Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10091)
*Shruti Agarwal,Vishal Asnani,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练方法，使不可感知的视觉水印能够与其它独立训练的水印系统共存。研究表明，虽然图像和视频水印已展现出天然的共存特性，但作者通过引入解码器感知的训练目标，显式优化多种水印的共存能力，从而提升解码鲁棒性和视觉质量，支持分层内容溯源认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的研究发现独立训练的图像水印模型可以共存（即水印集成），但这种共存是偶然性质而非显式优化目标，导致干扰不可控，可能损害解码鲁棒性或视觉质量。作者希望将这种共存从偶然性提升为可训练的、可控的优化目标。

Method: 首先通过实验证明共存特性同样适用于视频水印领域，然后提出一种解码器感知（decoder-aware）的训练目标，使图像和视频水印在训练过程中显式优化与其他水印系统的共存能力，从而减少相互干扰。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论是图像还是视频水印，采用解码器感知目标训练后，多种水印系统共存时的解码鲁棒性和视觉质量均得到改善，优于未显式优化共存的水印方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为分层溯源水印（layered provenance signaling）提供了可行路径：通过可训练的水印共存技术，可以实现独立部署的水印系统叠加使用，支持内容真实性和权利的分层验证。

Abstract: We present a method for training imperceptible visual watermarks to coexist with other such watermarks. Recent work has shown that independently trained image watermarking models can coexist with surprisingly limited interference, enabling watermark ensembling. However, this coexistence is a serendipitous property rather than an explicit optimization objective, leaving interference uncontrolled and potentially reducing decoding robustness or visual quality. We first show empirically that the same coexistence property extends to video watermarking. We then show that both image and video watermarks can be trained with a decoder-aware objective to improve coexistence. Our results suggest a practical path to signpost watermarks that indicate the presence of independently deployed provenance watermarking systems, supporting layered provenance signaling for content authenticity and rights.

</details>


### [5] [4D-WAM: 4D Consistent World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10107)
*Jiacheng Fu,Yibo Yuan,Meng Tian,Yue Li,Jiangtong Zhu,Jianhua Han,Yueyi Zhang,Jianwu Fang,Jianru Xue,Hang Xu,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM，通过几何基础模型在训练时提供4D一致性监督，使世界-动作模型（WAMs）在自动驾驶中能理解并预测物理一致的4D场景，同时提出决策导向的时间步采样策略改进轨迹规划，在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）仅基于2D视频投影训练，缺乏对底层4D驾驶场景结构的理解能力，导致生成的未来预测虽视觉上合理但4D不一致，进而误导下游规划模块。

Method: 1) 将WAM预测的未来帧输入几何基础模型，利用4D感知响应定义4D一致性损失函数，训练时引导模型理解、表示和预测物理一致的4D场景，且不增加推理成本；2) 识别WAMs中的"早期决策"现象，提出决策导向的时间步采样策略，强调在高噪声的早期阶段提供监督，因为驾驶决策主要在该阶段形成。

Result: 4D-WAM在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2基准上有效建模4D一致的场景演化并取得最先进性能，验证了4D一致性监督和决策导向采样策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过训练时的几何基础模型监督，WAMs能够获得4D场景结构理解能力，从而产生物理一致的未来预测并改善轨迹规划；决策形成的关键阶段需要针对性的监督策略。

Abstract: Emerging World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated promising performance in autonomous driving by jointly modeling future driving scene evolution and trajectory planning. However, existing WAMs are typically trained with video data, which is only 2D projections of the underlying 4D driving scene. Consequently, WAMs fail to understand and capture the structure of 4D scenes and thus generate visually plausible yet 4D inconsistent future predictions that mislead downstream planning. To alleviate this issue, we present 4D-WAM, a model that leverages geometric foundation models for training-time supervision to enable 4D consistent world modeling. Specifically, we feed WAM-predicted future frames into a geometric foundation model, and use 4D-aware responses to define a 4D consistency loss. This loss encourages the model to understand, represent, and predict physically consistent 4D scenes during training, without additional inference cost. Moreover, we identify an early-decision phenomenon in WAMs and propose a decision-oriented timestep sampling strategy that emphasizes supervision at early, high-noise stages, where driving decisions are primarily formed. By propagating 4D supervision to this critical decision-formation phase, the proposed strategy further improves trajectory planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that 4D-WAM effectively models 4D consistent scene evolution and achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [6] [P3CA: Encoder-Agnostic Interpretation of Vision Foundation Model Embeddings via Spatial Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10131)
*Amoon Jamzad,Dilakshan Srikanthan,Faranak Akbarifar,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P3CA（位置提示PCA），一种编码器无关的局部探测方法，用于检查医学图像基础模型生成的高维空间嵌入张量。通过用户选定的空间提示估计区域内的特征归一化和主协方差方向，再将投影应用到整个张量上，可视化局部信息性方向的表达情况。配合EmbedVision交互式工作流，在自然图像、结直肠病理基础模型嵌入和空间转录组张量上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型被广泛用作医学图像计算中的可复用编码器，但其高维空间嵌入难以检查——现有方法只能通过下游任务性能或全局降维（如PCA）来间接理解嵌入，无法在局部区域层面细致探查通道丰富的空间张量中包含的信息。

Method: P3CA：给定用户选择的空间提示区域，估计该区域内的特征归一化参数和主协方差方向（局部PCA），然后将所得投影应用于整个空间张量，生成区域条件化的表征透镜。该方法无需修改编码器、无需重训练、也不需要任务特定标签。此外实现了EmbedVision，一个基于3D Slicer的交互式工作流。

Result: 在自然图像、结直肠病理基础模型嵌入和空间转录组张量上，提示投影揭示了被全局PCA抑制的局部结构，改善了从冻结三维投影中进行的提示匹配病理判别，并支持学习表征与实测空间表征之间的比较。

Conclusion: P3CA提供了一种编码器无关、无需重训练和标签的局部嵌入检查方法，能够揭示全局方法遗漏的局部结构，具有跨领域的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are increasingly used as reusable encoders in medical image computing, yet their high-dimensional spatial embeddings are difficult to inspect beyond downstream task performance or global dimensionality reduction. We propose position-prompted PCA (P3CA), an encoder-agnostic method for local probing of channel-rich spatial tensors. Given a user-selected spatial prompt, P3CA estimates the feature normalization and dominant covariance directions within that region, then applies the resulting projection to the full tensor to visualize where locally informative directions are expressed. This produces a region-conditioned representation lens without modifying the encoder, retraining, or requiring task-specific labels. We implement P3CA in EmbedVision, an interactive 3D Slicer-based workflow, and evaluate it across natural images, colorectal pathology foundation-model embeddings, and spatial transcriptomic tensors. Across these settings, prompted projections reveal local structure suppressed by global PCA, improve prompt-matched pathology discrimination from frozen three-dimensional projections, and support comparison between learned and measured spatial representations.

</details>


### [7] [MAD-HOI: Masked Autoregressive Diffusion for Generating Articulated Hand Object Interactions from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10162)
*Ananya Bal,Kartik Sharma,Ethan Lai,Samyak Tiwari,Liza Dahiya,Chaitanya Chawla,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAD-HOI是一种将掩码自回归与扩散模型相结合的方法，用于手-物体交互（HOI）序列生成，能够在连续潜在空间中同时支持变长生成、复合运动序列、运动补全与插值，以及可靠的终止预测（EOM），在ARCTIC和GRAB数据集上表现出更优的多样性和物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本驱动手-物体交互序列生成方法主要关注平滑和物理合理的轨迹，但大多缺乏对变长生成、复合序列、运动补全和自动终止等实用功能的支持。标准扩散模型需要预先指定运动长度且只能处理原子运动；自回归方法虽具备序列级灵活性，但依赖于离散运动编码，会丢失接触敏感的运动细节。

Method: MAD-HOI首先将手与物体的运动编码到连续潜在空间中，并保持两者解耦以实现流级控制；随后使用掩码自回归Transformer预测上下文特征，这些特征用于条件化流匹配（flow-matching）头部模型；模型通过单一训练目标同时支持原子与复合关节运动生成、条件运动补全与插值，以及EOM（运动结束）预测。

Result: 在ARCTIC和GRAB数据集上的综合评估表明，MAD-HOI相比其他开源基线方法，能够生成更多样且物理更合理的手-物体交互。实验覆盖了运动生成、补全、插值以及EOM预测等多项能力。

Conclusion: MAD-HOI通过将掩码自回归与流匹配扩散相结合，在连续潜在空间中统一了多种HOI生成任务，解决了现有方法在序列级灵活性和接触细节保真之间的权衡问题，为文本驱动的手-物体交互生成提供了更具实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: Methods for text-based generation of hand-object interaction (HOI) sequences primarily focus on producing smooth, physically plausible trajectories. A truly utilitarian method should additionally support variable-length generation, composite motion sequences, motion completion and infilling, and reliable termination without compromising physical plausibility. Standard diffusion models for HOI generation are typically trained only for text-to-motion generation on atomic motions and require the motion length to be specified a-priori. Autoregressive (AR) methods provide greater sequence-level flexibility, but commonly depend on discrete motion codes, which can lose contact-sensitive motion detail. To address these key limitations, we present a model performing Masked Autoregression with Diffusion for HOI generation (MAD-HOI). Our method starts by encoding hand and object motions in a continuous latent space while keeping them disentangled to maintain stream-wise control. This is followed by a masked autoregressive transformer to predict context features that condition a flow-matching head. MAD-HOI is capable of motion generation for atomic and composite articulated sequences, conditioned motion completion and infilling, as well as EOM (End of Motion) prediction from a single training objective. We provide comprehensive evaluations for these capabilities and benchmark our method on the ARCTIC and GRAB datasets. Our experiments demonstrate that our method generates more diverse and physically plausible interactions compared to other open-sourced baseline methods.

</details>


### [8] [Motion Artifact-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning for 3D Brain MRI Motion Artifact Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10170)
*Mojtaba Safari,Shansong Wang,Zach Eidex,Matthew Goette,Tonghe Wang,Zhen Tian,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSRL-MAR，一个无需配对训练数据或显式运动标签的3D脑部MRI运动伪影去除框架，通过三阶段训练策略（对比学习提取运动表征、运动伪影感知合成网络、运动伪影感知生成器）实现自监督运动矫正，在in-silico和in-vivo数据集上均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者运动导致的脑部MRI图像退化（信号丢失、模糊、几何畸变）严重影响定量分析。现有深度学习方法依赖配对干净-损坏数据或k-space采集，这些在临床场景中难以获取，因此迫切需要一种无需配对数据的图像域运动伪影去除方法。

Method: SSRL-MAR采用三阶段训练策略：(1)在3D patch上进行对比学习，通过对比干净图像和合成损坏图像提取运动表征；(2)构建运动伪影感知合成网络，从干净扫描生成运动伪影；(3)利用学习到的退化器提供自监督监督信号，训练运动伪影感知生成器恢复干净体数据。整个过程无需配对数据或显式运动标签。

Result: 在in-silico数据集上达到PSNR 23.81dB、SSIM 91.55%、NMSE 0.79%。在in-vivo MR-ART数据集上，无监督域自适应进一步提升了解剖保真度；相比仅源监督模型，PSNR提升最高达2.0dB，与需要真实配对数据的oracle监督模型仅差0.25-0.47dB。轻度运动水平下，胼胝体和脑室系统等结构的体积误差降低超过50%。

Conclusion: SSRL-MAR提供了一种鲁棒且可扩展的图像域3D脑部MRI运动矫正方案，无需前瞻性获取的配对数据或采集特定校准，可在大规模神经影像研究中实现可靠的结构定量分析。

Abstract: Patient motion remains a source of image degradation in brain MRI, leading to signal loss, blurring, and geometric distortion that compromise quantitative analysis. Existing deep learning methods for motion correction typically rely on paired clean-corrupted data or k-space acquisitions, which are rarely available in clinical settings. We propose SSRL-MAR, a motion artifact-aware unpaired representation learning framework for motion artifact reduction that requires neither paired training data nor explicit motion labels. SSRL-MAR employed a three-stage training strategy: (1) contrastive learning on 3D patches to extract motion representations by contrasting clean and synthetically corrupted images, (2) a motion artifact-aware synthesis network to generate motion artifacts from clean scans, and (3) a motion artifact-aware generator to restore clean volumes using the learned degrader for self-supervised supervision. On in-silico dataset, SSRL-MAR achieved PSNR 23.81dB, SSIM 91.55%, and NMSE 0.79%. On in-vivo MR-ART dataset, the pretrained model reduced motion distortion, and unsupervised domain adaptation further improved anatomical fidelity. Against a source-only supervised model trained on the same simulated pairs, SSRL-MAR improved PSNR by up to 2.0 dB on MR-ART after unsupervised domain adaptation, and remained within 0.25-0.47 dB of an oracle supervised model that requires real paired data unavailable in practice. At the milder motion level, volumetric error in structures such as the corpus callosum and ventricular system decreased by more than 50%, confirming improved neuroanatomical consistency. These results indicate that SSRL-MAR provides a robust and scalable image-domain solution for 3D brain MRI motion correction, enabling reliable structural quantification in large-scale neuroimaging studies without requiring prospectively acquired pairs or acquisition-specific calibration.

</details>


### [9] [DoseBridge: Denoising Diffusion Bridge Model for Dose Prediction in Lung Intensity-Modulated Proton Therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10173)
*Zerun Zhang,Xiaoda Cong,Xiangkun Xu,Peter Y. Chen,Xuanfeng Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DoseBridge是一个基于去噪扩散桥模型(denoising diffusion bridge model)的放射治疗剂量预测框架，将患者CT作为结构化端点、束流几何编码为空间对齐的束流掩码，并通过多尺度融合整合CT、靶区、危及器官及束流掩码信息。在52例晚期肺癌质子治疗(IMPT)回顾性队列中，DoseBridge在MAE(4.170 Gy)、PSNR(23.06 dB)和SSIM(0.798)上均优于两个对比深度学习模型，且能通过仅改变束流掩码重定向低剂量入射区域。这是首个用于放疗剂量预测的去噪扩散桥模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放疗剂量预测模型大多仅使用CT图像和解剖结构，忽略了IMPT剂量对束流几何的强依赖性；同时临床可用数据集通常较小，传统深度学习方法在此场景下性能受限。因此需要一种能够编码束流几何信息且适用于小样本数据的剂-量预测方法。

Method: 提出DoseBridge框架：1) 采用去噪扩散桥模型(denoising diffusion bridge)，以患者CT作为结构化桥接端点；2) 将计划特定的束流几何编码为空间对齐的束流掩码(beam mask)；3) 多尺度融合(fusion)整合CT、靶区(target)、危及器官(OAR)和束流掩码表示，仅增加1.95%额外参数；4) 在42例训练、10例测试的晚期肺癌IMPT(60 Gy/30次)数据集上进行回顾性评估。

Result: 在测试队列上，DoseBridge达到平均绝对误差4.170 Gy、峰值信噪比23.06 dB、结构相似性指数0.798，均优于两个对比模型。CTV D95与参考剂量的差异为0.62±1.6 Gy；OAR平均剂量符号差异为-0.32至0.24 Gy；急性食管炎和放射性肺炎的NTCP差异分别为-0.40±2.2和0.52±3.4个百分点。仅改变束流掩码即可重定向低剂量入射区域，同时保持高剂量靶区不变。

Conclusion: DoseBridge是首个用于放疗剂量预测的去噪扩散桥模型，证明了其作为束流感知的肺癌IMPT规划先验(feasibility as a beam-aware planning prior)的可行性。未来需要在更大规模外部队列中进行验证。

Abstract: Most radiotherapy dose-prediction models use only CT images and anatomical structures, although intensity-modulated proton therapy (IMPT) dose also depends strongly on beam geometry and available clinical datasets are often small. We present DoseBridge, a denoising diffusion bridge model that uses the patient CT as a structured bridge endpoint and encodes plan-specific beam geometry in a spatially aligned beam mask. Multiscale fusion combines CT, target, organ-at-risk, and beam-mask representations with 1.95% additional parameters. DoseBridge was retrospectively evaluated on single-institution CT images and treatment plans from 52 patients with advanced-stage lung cancer treated with 60 Gy in 30 fractions; 42 cases were used for training and 10 for testing. Performance was assessed using image-similarity, dose-volume, and Lyman-Kutcher-Burman normal-tissue complication probability (NTCP) metrics and compared with two deep-learning models. On the test cohort, DoseBridge achieved a mean absolute error of 4.170 Gy, peak signal-to-noise ratio of 23.06 dB, and structural similarity index of 0.798, outperforming both comparison models on these metrics. Clinical target volume D95 differed from the reference dose by 0.62 +/- 1.6 Gy; signed organ-at-risk mean-dose differences ranged from -0.32 to 0.24 Gy, and NTCP differences were -0.40 +/- 2.2 and 0.52 +/- 3.4 percentage points for acute esophagitis and radiation pneumonitis, respectively. Changing only the beam mask redirected predicted low-dose entrance regions while preserving the high-dose target region. To our knowledge, DoseBridge is the first denoising diffusion bridge model for radiotherapy dose prediction. These results support its feasibility as a beam-aware planning prior for lung IMPT, pending evaluation in larger external cohorts.

</details>


### [10] [Human versus Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10181)
*Elena Sirotkina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于3023名美国成年人、1140万个真实网络摄像头注视点数据，评估了计算机视觉显著性模型的预测能力。结果显示，未经训练的"画面中心"标记优于所有训练过的网络模型，且剩余精度存在系统性偏差——偏向年轻、白人和温和派观众，歧视年长、黑人和意识形态极端的观众。作者提出了一种基于群体真实注视行为来判断模型能否学习该群体的新方法，并跨所有人口统计维度验证，为"系统能否看见所有人"提供了评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显著性模型被广泛应用于预测观众注意力，且已成为数十亿美元的"预测注意力"产业。但这些模型很少从真实观众的视角进行大规模验证，其在不同人口群体上的公平性和有效性缺乏严格检验。

Method: 招募3023名符合全国人口配额的美国成年人，观看流通中的新闻照片，采集1140万个网络摄像头注视点；将领先的显著性模型与未训练的中央标记进行对比测试，并分析模型精度偏差与人口特征（年龄、种族、意识形态）之间的关系；进一步提出基于群体自身注视行为来判断模型可学习性的框架，并在各人口维度上进行验证。

Result: 未训练的中央标记胜过所有训练过的网络；模型剩余精度具有系统性偏差，偏向年轻、白人和温和派观众，对年长、黑人和意识形态极端观众表现更差。

Conclusion: 论文提出了从"观众侧"评估显著性模型的新标准，揭示了现有模型在真实人群中的局限性和不公平性，并提出一条让模型学会"看见所有人"的前进路径。

Abstract: Computer vision saliency models predict where people will look, one map per image, and a billion-dollar predicted-attention industry sells those maps in place of measuring real viewers. I test the leading models from the audience side, against 11.4 million webcam gaze points from 3,023 US adults recruited to national quotas, viewing circulating news photographs. I show that an untrained central marker outperforms every trained network, because the content the networks add on top of the center falls where these audiences never look. What accuracy remains is systematically biased, favoring younger, White, and moderate viewers over older, Black, and ideologically extreme ones. I propose a way forward and build on what a group's own gaze reveals about whether a model can learn that group at all, and I apply it across every demographic axis this sample supports. Ultimately, I show how systems that decide what people see can learn to see everyone, and this study supplies the standard by which such a claim should be judged.

</details>


### [11] [More Accurate, Less Human: Gestalt Grouping in Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10195)
*Sudhanva Manjunath Athreya,Sai Phani Kumar Malladi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套行为测试组合（behavioral battery），利用已有的人类感知实验数据来评估视觉模型是否具备与人类一致的完形（Gestalt）组织能力。测试涵盖四种分组任务，应用于45个来自五个训练家族的模型，发现与人类反应的对齐程度能揭示传统基准指标无法区分的感知组织差异，为可视化研究提供了无需新增用户研究的可复用评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉系统将视觉内容组织为整体（如相同颜色的点聚成系列、相似标记归为类别、形状完形为可识别对象），可视化设计正是建立在这样的完形操作之上。然而，视觉模型是否以这种方式组织视觉内容尚未被系统测试过，传统性能指标（如基准准确率）无法区分模型在感知组织层面的差异。

Method: 作者引入了一个行为测试组合（behavioral battery），基于先前人类感知研究的数据对模型进行评分，涵盖四种分组任务：标记颜色异类检测（mark-color odd-one-out）、颜色系列计数（color-series counting）、剪影识别（silhouette recognition）和对象异类检测（object odd-one-out）。作者将该测试应用于45个模型，覆盖五个训练家族：监督学习、自监督学习、对比视觉-语言编码器、开源权重VLM以及闭源基础模型。

Result: 测试组合揭示出，与人类反应的一致性能够捕捉到传统性能指标无法区分的感知组织方面。部分闭源模型的感知组织对齐程度显著低于其基准准确率所暗示的水平。

Conclusion: 基于已发表感知数据进行评分，为可视化研究提供了一个可重复使用的评估标尺，无需进行新的用户研究，即可审计进入可视化流程的模型是否以与其人类受众相同的方式组织视觉内容。

Abstract: Human vision organizes what it sees into wholes: same-colored points group into series, similar marks cohere into categories, and shapes complete into recognizable objects. These are the Gestalt operations that visualization design builds on. Whether vision models organize visual content this way has not been systematically tested. We introduce a behavioral battery that scores models against human data from prior perception studies on four grouping tasks: mark-color odd-one-out, color-series counting, silhouette recognition, and object odd-one-out. We apply it to 45 models across five training families: supervised, self-supervised, and contrastive vision-language encoders, open-weight VLMs, and closed foundation models. The battery reveals that agreement with human responses captures aspects of perceptual organization that conventional performance metrics fail to distinguish, with several closed models exhibiting substantially lower alignment than their benchmark accuracy would suggest. Scoring against published perception data therefore gives visualization research a reusable yardstick, requiring no new user study, for auditing whether the models now entering visualization pipelines organize what they see the way their human audience does.

</details>


### [12] [A Convolutional Layer Activation Dimensionality Reduction for Out-of-Distribution and Adversarial Attack Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10203)
*Leandro de Souza Rosa,Lorenzo Capelli,Clara Nunes Barrancos,Mauro Mangia,Riccardo Rovatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控高压缩的卷积层降维方法，用于改进基于中间激活的OOD和对抗攻击检测方法，在保持或提升检测性能的同时大幅降低计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络在图像分类和多模态模型中应用广泛，但其对OOD样本和对抗攻击的脆弱性引发安全担忧。基于中间激活置信度估计的检测方法依赖于降维步骤，然而现有卷积层降维方法要么缺乏对压缩/信息损失权衡的控制，要么产生较大的表示，无法高效支持后续处理。

Method: 本文首先仔细分析了两种最先进的检测方法及其在卷积层上的降维策略，随后开发了一种新的可控高压缩降维方法。同时扩展了这两种检测方法，使其能够适配任意降维方式，并在OOD和对抗攻击检测任务上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，采用所提出降维方法的检测方法在各种设定下一致优于或与最强基线相当；同时，由于所提方法在所有对比方法中压缩率最高，显著降低了计算和内存占用。

Conclusion: 所提出的可控高压缩卷积层降维方法在保持检测性能的同时实现了更高的压缩效率，为基于中间激活的OOD和对抗攻击检测提供了一种更高效、更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Despite the success of convolutional neural networks in image classification tasks and their general application in multi-modal models, their susceptibility to out-of-distribution and adversarial attack samples raises concerns regarding trustworthiness and safety. Among the approaches to tackle such issues, detection methods that analyze the model's intermediate activations to estimate a confidence score are a promising family that evaluates the decision process, relying on a dimensionality reduction step to enable efficient downstream processing of the high-dimensional activations. However, when considering convolutional layers, the dimensionality reduction methods in the literature either lack a mechanism to control the compression/information-loss trade-off or yield large representations. In this paper, we carefully analyze two state-of-the-art detection methods and their dimensionality reductions for convolutional layers and develop a novel reduction method with a controllable high-compression level. We extend these two state-of-the-art detection methods, enabling the usage of any dimensionality reduction, and evaluate their performance on out-of-distribution and adversarial attack detection. Results show that the detection methods with the proposed dimensionality reduction consistently perform better than, or comparable to, the strongest alternative. Furthermore, the proposed method is shown to reduce computation and memory footprints, given that it has the highest compression among the compared methods.

</details>


### [13] [Chain of Spatial Thoughts: Modality-Agnostic Spatial Grounding for Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10278)
*Hunter Schofield,Mohammed Elmahgiubi,Mohammad Mahdavian,Richard Shi,Jinjun Shan,Amir Rasouli,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Space Tokens，一种轻量级且与架构无关的框架，通过将场景级3D几何和物体中心的空间属性蒸馏为连续潜在token，并将其直接融入链式思考推理过程，在不引入额外推理时模块的情况下增强VLM的空间推理能力。在VSI-Bench上，Qwen3-VL-8B提升4.3%，SenseNova-SI-1.3提升1.3%，物体大小估计达到79.2%、房间大小估计达到75.7%的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间理解对于具身智能至关重要，涵盖机器人操作、具身导航和自动驾驶等应用。尽管现有VLM在空间推理基准上表现优异，但SOTA方法通常在推理时依赖额外的空间编码器或架构修改，导致计算成本增加。作者旨在开发一种无需额外推理时模块、计算高效且可解释的空间表示机制。

Method: 提出Space Tokens框架：首先将场景级3D几何和物体中心的空间属性（如物体大小、房间大小等）蒸馏为连续潜在token；这些token被直接融入VLM的链式思考推理过程，作为显式空间表示参与推理；同时学习到的表示可被显式解码以验证其编码了有意义的几何信息；统一的token接口支持扩展到其他模态。

Result: 在VSI-Bench基准上，Qwen3-VL-8B提升4.3%，SenseNova-SI-1.3提升1.3%；在物体大小估计上达到79.2%、房间大小估计上达到75.7%的最先进性能，证明了连续空间token的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 连续空间token为大型视觉语言模型提供了有效、可解释且计算高效的几何推理集成机制，在不需要额外推理时模块的情况下显著提升空间理解能力，且框架具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is fundamental to embodied intelligence, underpinning applications such as robotic manipulation, embodied navigation, and autonomous driving. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance on spatial reasoning benchmarks, state-of-the-art approaches typically rely on additional spatial encoders or architectural modifications during inference, increasing computational cost. We introduce Space Tokens, a lightweight, architecture-agnostic framework that equips VLMs with explicit continuous spatial representations without requiring additional inference-time modules. By distilling scene-level 3D geometry and object-centric spatial attributes into continuous latent tokens, our method enables these modalities to be directly incorporated into a chain-of-thought reasoning process, thereby improving the VLM's spatial reasoning capabilities. At the same time, the learned representations can be explicitly decoded to verify that they encode meaningful geometric information, while the unified token interface remains extensible to additional modalities. Experiments on VSI-Bench improve Qwen3-VL-8B by 4.3% and SenseNova-SI-1.3 by 1.3%, while achieving state-of-the-art performance on object size (79.2%) and room size estimation (75.7%). These results demonstrate that continuous spatial tokens provide an effective, interpretable, and computationally efficient mechanism for integrating geometric reasoning into large vision-language models.

</details>


### [14] [TRACE-GS: On-Policy Trajectory Distillation with Privileged Geometric Conditioning for Sparse-View 3DGS Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10286)
*Linlian Jiang,Yuchen Xi,Sadman Rakib Pinon,Ruigang Yang,Yang Wang,Xinxin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE-GS是一个基于on-policy轨迹蒸馏的稀疏视角3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)修复框架，通过训练时利用特权几何条件，将扩散先验适配到稀疏视角3DGS修复任务上，在部署时仅保留学生模型，取得了跨数据集和稀疏视角设置的一致改进与强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的稀疏视角3DGS修复方法存在一个根本限制：在独立加噪状态下进行的监督无法覆盖推理阶段实际到达的状态。由于稀疏视角下几何约束不足，去噪从开始就存在偏差，且这些偏差在rollout过程中逐步累积放大。作者认为不应追求更复杂的修复架构，而应解决这一训练-推理分布不匹配的根本问题。

Method: TRACE-GS采用on-policy轨迹蒸馏策略：以额外训练视角提供更丰富几何信息的教师模型，在稀疏视角学生模型自身的rollout路径上提供监督目标，对齐每个访问状态下的去噪方向和跨视角响应。这种仅存在于训练阶段的几何信息使其处于LUPI（Learning Using Privileged Information）设置下。部署时仅保留稀疏视角学生模型，将其修复后的渲染结果作为伪观测用于3DGS细化。

Result: TRACE-GS是首个从特权几何中为稀疏视角3DGS修复推导on-policy监督的方法，在多个数据集和不同稀疏视角设置下实现了一致的性能提升和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过识别现有扩散方法中训练-推理状态分布不匹配这一核心问题，并利用特权几何信息在on-policy轨迹上进行蒸馏，TRACE-GS有效解决了稀疏视角3DGS修复中的偏差累积问题，验证了LUPI范式在此任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present TRACE-GS, an on-policy trajectory distillation framework that leverages privileged geometric conditioning at training time, thereby adapting a diffusion prior to sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) restoration. Rather than pursuing increasingly sophisticated restoration architectures, we identify a more fundamental limitation shared by existing diffusion-based approaches: supervision at independently noised states does not cover those reached during inference. In sparse-view 3DGS, under-constrained geometry biases denoising from the outset, and the resulting deviations compound along the rollout. TRACE-GS instead performs on-policy trajectory distillation: a teacher conditioned on richer geometry from additional training views supplies targets along the sparse-view student's own rollout, aligning denoising directions and cross-view responses at each visited state. This training-only geometry places TRACE-GS in the learning using privileged information (LUPI) setting. At deployment, only the sparse-view student is retained, and its restored renderings serve as pseudo-observations for 3DGS refinement. To the best of our knowledge, TRACE-GS is the first to derive on-policy supervision from privileged geometry for sparse-view 3DGS restoration, achieving consistent gains and strong generalization across datasets and sparse-view settings.

</details>


### [15] [SeFaR: Semantic Feature-aware Robustness Testing of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10289)
*Nusrat Jahan Mozumder,Divya Gopinath,Corina Pasareanu,Matthew Dwyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeFaR框架，用于系统性地测试视觉模型的语义鲁棒性。通过分层概念模型和扩散模型/视觉语言模型，生成保持需求满足的光照级语义扰动，并采用自适应反馈过程发现导致失败的语义概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域被广泛部署为感知模块，其故障常源于罕见且代表性不足的场景。现有方法缺乏对感知模型语义鲁棒性的系统评估，即模型行为对真实世界感知变异与高层需求的一致性验证。

Method: SeFaR框架包含：(1) 新颖的分层概念模型，实现特征空间的结构化探索并支持用户定义领域的知识注入；(2) 利用最先进的扩散模型和视觉语言模型生成保持语义的逼真扰动；(3) 识别影响行为的先前未知特征；(4) 采用反馈驱动的自适应过程，生成可解释的失败诱导语义概念及相应测试输入。

Result: 在多个案例研究上的评估表明，该框架能有效满足需求前置条件，同时识别影响模型决策但与需求无关的特征，既能发现故障又能将这些故障关联到特定特征。

Conclusion: SeFaR提供了一种系统化、可解释的语义特征中心测试方法，能够在满足原始需求约束的前提下发现并归因视觉感知模型的鲁棒性缺陷，为安全关键领域中的模型验证提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains as perception modules, where failures are often caused due to rare and under-represented scenarios. This necessitates the need to evaluate the semantic robustness of perception models; conformance of behavior to high-level requirements over real-world perceptual variability. To address this, we propose SeFaR, a framework for systematic semantic-feature-centric testing of vision models. Given a natural-language requirement and a set of satisfying inputs, SeFaR evaluates robustness with respect to diverse realistic semantic variations that preserve requirement satisfaction. The approach employs a novel hierarchical concept model enabling structured exploration of the feature space and incorporation of domain knowledge via user-defined concepts. State-of-the-art diffusion and vision-language models are leveraged to generate photorealistic semantics-preserving perturbations and identification of previously unknown features impacting behavior. A feedback-driven adaptive process is adopted to generate interpretable failure-inducing semantic concepts along with corresponding test inputs. Evaluation on case studies demonstrates that the proposed framework effectively satisfies requirement preconditions while identifying requirement-independent features that influence model decisions, enabling it to both uncover faults and relate them to such features.

</details>


### [16] [MRIComp4Flow: Compression of 3D Brain MRI for Training Multi-Modal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10291)
*Lisa K. Fischer,Mykhailo Riabets,Daniel Rueckert,Benedikt Wiestler,Anke Meyer-Baese,Sandeep Nagar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模多模态MRI数据集的存储与I/O开销问题，探索使用JPEG2000和JPEG-LS等标准图像编码器对有脑肿瘤MRI进行有损压缩后，是否仍能保持3D生成模型的合成质量。实验表明在20:1压缩比下，基于Wavelet Flow Matching训练的模型与未压缩数据训练的模型合成质量统计等效（PSNR和SSIM均在预设裕度内），证明JPEG2000压缩是迈向可扩展3D MRI生成建模的实用步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多模态MRI数据集带来巨大的存储和I/O成本，限制了在普通硬件上训练3D生成模型。虽然已知有损压缩对判别性分割网络的精度影响较小，但生成模型需要学习完整数据分布而非决策边界，压缩对其影响尚未被探索。

Method: 使用JPEG2000或近无损JPEG-LS流水线对每个3D体数据进行压缩；在压缩数据上训练以BraTS图像序列（T1n, T1c, T2, T2f）为条件的Wavelet Flow Matching模型，并在验证集上评估模型表现。

Result: 在20:1压缩比下，合成质量与未压缩数据训练的模型统计等效（ΔPSNR<1dB, ΔSSIM<0.02，配对TOST检验p值满足要求）：各模态平均PSNR为27.3dB vs 27.0dB，平均SSIM为0.95 vs 0.96。

Conclusion: JPEG2000压缩是迈向可扩展3D MRI生成建模的实用步骤，在不降低合成质量的前提下有效缓解存储和I/O瓶颈。代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-modal MRI datasets impose substantial storage and I/O costs, limiting the training of 3D generative models on commodity infrastructure. While lossy compression is known to preserve accuracy for discriminative segmentation networks, its effect on generative models, which must learn the full data distribution rather than a decision boundary, is unexplored. We study whether standard image codecs can effectively compress semantically rich brain tumor MRI while preserving the fidelity required to train and deploy a 3D MRI generative model. Each 3D volume is compressed with JPEG2000 or a near-lossless JPEG-LS pipeline. Next, a Wavelet Flow Matching model, conditioned on BraTS image sequences (T1n, T1c, T2, T2f), is trained on compressed data, and the resulting models are evaluated on the validation set. At a 20:1 compression ratio, synthesis quality is statistically equivalent to a model trained on uncompressed data within a pre-specified margin ($Δ$PSNR $<1$,dB, $Δ$SSIM $<0.02$; paired TOST $p=[[p]]$): mean PSNR is 27.3,dB vs. 27.0,dB and mean SSIM is 0.95 vs. 0.96 across modalities. Our results indicate that JPEG2000 compression is a practical step toward scalable 3D MRI generative modeling without degrading synthesis quality. The codebase is available at https://github.com/lisafis/MRIComp4Flow .

</details>


### [17] [Frozen Brain-MRI Foundation Models Are Site Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10295)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了冻结基础模型（FM）在脑MRI上的嵌入表示，发现采集站点是一个内在且大尺度的表征成分：在多个队列、多种编码器（含随机初始化）和所有网络深度下，站点均能以约0.9的平衡准确率进行线性解码，效果源于低层图像统计而非预训练。站点子空间可通过迭代零空间投影或ComBat移除（解码准确率0.94→0.07/0.00），但由于站点与解剖共用纠缠线性子空间，移除会损害密集分割性能。作者建议对冻结脑MRI-FM进行站点审计，并开源审计工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结基础模型嵌入被广泛用作脑MRI的现成表征，前提假设是它们捕捉了解剖结构。作者质疑这一假设，希望审计这些嵌入实际编码了什么，特别是关注采集站点等非解剖混杂因素是否内在地主导了表征。

Method: 在两个独立队列（ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II）上，使用三种冻结3D编码器（脑预训练、CT预训练、随机初始化）和三种架构家族（Swin、ViT、ResNet），在每个网络深度下评估站点、性别、年龄、自闭症诊断的线性可解码性。同时测试了原始下采样图像的站点可解码性、人口协变量残差化后的变化、非线性探针与线性探针的对比，以及通过迭代零空间投影和ComBat进行站点子空间移除对密集分割的影响。

Result: 站点在所有设置下均可线性解码至约0.9的平衡准确率，且随机初始化编码器（~0.9）和原始下采样图像（~0.95）均具备该能力，表明指纹源于低层图像统计而非预训练。残差化人口协变量后站点可解码性不变，说明是采集而非人群驱动。非线性探针与线性探针结果一致。站点子空间可被后验移除（0.94→0.07/0.00），但移除会破坏密集分割（匹配秩随机方向投影Dice中性，而站点子空间移除具破坏性），因为站点与解剖子空间纠缠。

Conclusion: 冻结脑MRI基础模型的嵌入中，采集站点是内在且占主导的成分，对共享或联邦嵌入构成站点归因风险。应使用站点审计的冻结脑MRI-FM，且后验移除站点子空间对分割任务并非无代价。作者开源了审计工具包以便社区进行此类审计。

Abstract: Frozen foundation-model (FM) embeddings are increasingly used as off-the-shelf brain-MRI representations, on the assumption that they capture anatomy. We audit what they actually encode and find that acquisition site is a large, intrinsic component of the representation. Across two independent cohorts (ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II), three frozen 3-D encoders (brain-pretrained, CT-pretrained, and randomly initialized), and every network depth, site is linearly decodable at roughly 0.9 balanced accuracy at deep layers, exceeding the decodability of every clinical or demographic variable (sex, age, autism diagnosis) at every layer. The effect is intrinsic rather than learned: a randomly initialized encoder is already a ~0.9 site classifier on both cohorts and across three architecture families (Swin, ViT, ResNet), and site is decodable at ~0.95 directly from the raw downsampled image with no encoder, so the fingerprint reflects low-level image statistics that any encoder preserves rather than a product of pretraining. Residualizing measured population covariates leaves site decodability essentially unchanged, indicating an acquisition- rather than population-driven effect. A nonlinear probe matches the linear one, so the fingerprint is fully linearly accessible. The site subspace is removable post hoc by iterative null-space projection or ComBat (site decodability 0.94 -> 0.07/0.00), and is a site-attribution concern for shared or federated embeddings; but for dense segmentation this removal is not free, because site and anatomy occupy an entangled linear subspace (a matched-rank random-direction projection is Dice-neutral, whereas removing the site subspace is destructive). We recommend site-audited use of frozen brain-MRI FMs and release an open audit toolkit.

</details>


### [18] [UniMod: Enhancing Multi-Modal Medical Diagnosis through Cross-Modality and Within-Modality Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10316)
*Zijian Gu,Weikai Lin,Shuang Zhou,Zihan Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMod是一个用于医学多模态学习（图像+临床文本）的框架，通过同时监督图像-only、文本-only和多模态分类，并要求每个模态独立预测诊断来缓解快捷学习（shortcut learning）问题。在哈佛青光眼数据集上达到0.850 AUC，在CheXpert Plus上达到0.966 AUC，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多模态训练存在快捷学习问题：模型倾向于利用更易学习的模态（如文本中的诊断线索）而忽略更难学习的特征（如图像中的细微视觉模式），导致模型未能充分从各模态中提取诊断信息。这限制了多模态学习在医学诊断中的实际效果。

Method: UniMod框架通过三种机制缓解快捷学习：(1)同时对图像-only、文本-only和多模态三条路径进行分类监督，迫使每个模态独立提取诊断特征；(2)引入跨模态对齐实现知识迁移；(3)对同一诊断标签的患者施加模态内的监督对比对齐，增强特征的判别性。

Result: 在Harvard-Glaucoma数据集上达到0.850 AUC，比OGM-GE和Gradient Blending高出1.6-1.8%；在CheXpert Plus上达到0.966 AUC，比现有方法高出超过5%。UniMod还能无需架构修改直接扩展到5类多标签诊断任务，比CGGM提升平均AUC 0.097。

Conclusion: 通过要求每个模态独立预测诊断并添加跨模态与模态内对齐，UniMod有效缓解了多模态医学学习中的快捷学习问题，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-modal learning combining medical images and clinical text is promising for disease diagnosis. However, standard multi-modal training leads to shortcut learning: models exploit the easier modality (e.g., diagnostic cues in text) while neglecting harder-to-learn features (e.g., subtle visual patterns). We propose UniMod, a framework that mitigates shortcut learning by requiring each modality to predict the diagnosis on its own. It supervises image-only, text-only, and multi-modal classification simultaneously, so each modality must extract diagnostic features. We add cross-modality alignment for knowledge transfer and within-modality supervised contrastive alignment over same-diagnosis patients. On Harvard-Glaucoma, UniMod reaches 0.850 AUC, outperforming OGM-GE and Gradient Blending by 1.6-1.8%; on CheXpert Plus, it reaches 0.966 AUC, surpassing them by over 5%. UniMod also extends to 5-class multi-label diagnosis without architectural change, improving mean AUC by 0.097 over CGGM.

</details>


### [19] [From Detection to Understanding: TAR and TAR-Bench for Multi-Task Traffic Anomaly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10317)
*Han Zhang,Yilin Zhao,Zaid Pervaiz Bhat,Zheng Tang,Varun Praveen,Vidya N. Murali,David C. Anastasiu,Tomasz Kornuta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAR（交通异常推理）数据集和TAR-Bench基准，用于训练和评估视频-语言模型在交通异常场景中的推理能力，超越了传统的异常检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通异常检测研究主要聚焦于二分类检测（是否异常），缺乏对异常事件深层推理（时间、空间、场景）的建模与评估。作者旨在推动视频-语言模型从简单的异常识别走向更复杂的推理能力，并提供一个大规模训练数据与高质量评估基准。

Method: TAR包含44,040条思维链（chain-of-thought）训练标注，覆盖10个任务，来源于8个公开数据集的3,670段CCTV视频（约26小时）。训练标注由MAVEN方法自动生成，该方法将多尺度视频证据整合为结构化事件描述，再生成问答对和推理轨迹。TAR-Bench包含960条人工精选测试标注，用于80段从17个YouTube视频中裁剪的保留片段。作者在TAR-Bench上评估了11个视频-语言模型，并进行了多任务微调实验。

Result: 实验表明，强问答精度并不能可靠预测模型的时间或场景推理能力，揭示了两者之间的能力差距。在TAR上进行多任务微调带来了一致性的性能提升，其中完整10任务模型相比零样本基线在综合得分上提升了21.4个百分点。

Conclusion: TAR和TAR-Bench为交通异常推理提供了首个大规模训练数据与高质量评估基准，并作为AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3的官方训练与领域内评估数据，推动视频-语言模型在交通场景推理方向的发展。

Abstract: We present TAR (Traffic Anomaly Reasoning) and TAR-Bench datasets, resources for training and evaluating video-language models beyond anomaly detection. TAR contains 44,040 chain-of-thought training annotations across 10 tasks for 3,670 CCTV videos ($\sim$26 hours) from eight public datasets. Its evaluation component, TAR-Bench, contains 960 human-curated test annotations for 80 held-out clips trimmed from 17 public YouTube videos. TAR's training annotations are produced with MAVEN, which consolidates multi-scale video evidence into structured event descriptions before generating question-answer pairs and reasoning traces. On TAR-Bench, eleven vision-language models reveal that strong question-answering accuracy does not reliably predict temporal or scene reasoning ability. Multi-task fine-tuning on TAR yields consistent gains, with the full 10-task model improving aggregate score by 21.4 points over its zero-shot baseline. TAR and TAR-Bench provide the official training and in-domain evaluation data for AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/nvidia/PhysicalAI-Traffic-Anomaly-Reasoning

</details>


### [20] [ENCORE: Efficient Noise Context-Aware Representation for Low-Dose CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10343)
*Minwoo Yu,N. Robert Bennett,Jongduk Baek,Adam S. Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个面向低剂量CT去噪的高效噪声上下文感知表示(ENCORE)框架，通过重构噪声合成过程、提取局部噪声功率与相关性上下文，并引入FlyingConv模块实现自适应卷积权重调节，在去噪质量和计算效率上均取得显著提升，同时支持零样本条件去噪以动态控制输出纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习CT去噪模型直接使用为自然图像设计的通用架构，未能考虑CT噪声的非平稳性和空间相关性特征，导致去噪效果受限。

Method: 1) 基于更真实的噪声分布（超越传统高斯近似）重构噪声合成过程，为训练配对生成建立严谨基础；2) 提取局部噪声功率和相关性上下文来引导去噪；3) 提出FlyingConv模块，根据每个局部图像区域自适应改变卷积权重。

Result: 在去噪质量和计算效率两方面均展现出显著提升；通过修改推理时噪声上下文图的强度，可实现零样本条件去噪，动态控制输出图像纹理。

Conclusion: 显式利用CT噪声特征和解剖结构信息能够显著提升去噪性能与效率，且噪声上下文的可操控性为条件去噪提供了灵活的新范式。

Abstract: While deep learning-based denoising has become widely adopted in low-dose CT, conventional models use generic architectures designed for natural images, failing to account for non-stationary and spatially correlated CT noise characteristics. To address this, we propose an Efficient Noise COntext-aware REpresentation (ENCORE) framework that explicitly leverages CT noise characteristics and anatomical features. First, we reformulate the noise synthesis procedure based on a realistic noise distribution beyond the conventional Gaussian approximation, establishing a rigorous foundation for training pair generation. Next, we extract local noise power and correlation contexts to guide the denoising process. To fully leverage the potential of noise context, we propose a FlyingConv module, which adaptively changes convolution weights for each local image region. Notably, our approach demonstrates substantial gains in both denoising quality and computational efficiency. Furthermore, manipulating the intensity of the noise context maps at inference time enables zero-shot conditional denoising, allowing for dynamic control over the output image texture. The entire pipeline is available at https://github.com/minwoo-yu/ENCORE.git

</details>


### [21] [CasDeblurGS: Cascaded 2D-to-3D Multi-View Consistency for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Two Blurry Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10345)
*Haeyun Choi,Minhyuk Jang,I-Gil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CasDeblurGS，一个级联框架，仅从两张运动模糊图像（已知内参、无位姿、无辅助清晰图、无测试时优化）重建连贯3D场景。方法通过两阶段：局部2D对应滤波提供局部引导，再聚合中间恢复结果构建无位姿3D高斯表示以提供全局引导，最终实现高质量新视角合成。在Deblur-NeRF数据集上PSNR提升1.19dB（真实）和2.11dB（合成）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由视角3D场景媒体对沉浸式应用日益重要，但实际拍摄常面临严重的视角稀疏和运动模糊问题。现有的模糊感知神经渲染方法要么需要大量多视角冗余、精确相机位姿，要么需要昂贵的逐场景优化，无法适应仅有两张运动模糊图像这一严苛但实际的场景。作者旨在解决这一极端稀疏且模糊条件下的3D场景重建问题。

Method: 提出CasDeblurGS级联框架，分两阶段渐进恢复跨视角信息。Stage 1通过遮挡感知的对应滤波构建局部可靠的2D引导；Stage 2将中间恢复结果聚合为无位姿的临时3D高斯表示，其输入视角重渲染提供密集的全局3D引导，用于最终恢复。整个过程无需输入视角位姿、辅助清晰图像或逐场景测试时优化。

Result: 在真实世界和合成的Deblur-NeRF场景上均一致优于强基线，PSNR分别提升1.19 dB和2.11 dB。渐进消融实验、跨视角对应可视化和相机重投影分析进一步证明渲染质量和多视角几何一致性均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过从局部2D对应到全局3D引导的级联递进策略，成功实现了仅用两张运动模糊图像即可重建连贯3D场景，验证了局部-全局引导递进范式在极端稀疏模糊条件下的有效性。

Abstract: Free-viewpoint 3D scene media is increasingly important for immersive applications, yet practical capture often suffers from severe view sparsity and motion blur. Although neural rendering has advanced sparse-view synthesis, existing blur-aware methods typically require substantial multi-view redundancy, accurate camera poses, or costly per-scene optimization. We address a stringent yet practical setting: reconstructing a coherent 3D scene from only two motion-blurred images with known intrinsics, without input-view poses, auxiliary sharp images, or per-scene test-time optimization. To this end, we propose CasDeblurGS, a cascaded framework that progressively recovers reliable cross-view information from local 2D correspondences to global 3D guidance. Stage 1 constructs locally reliable guidance through occlusion-aware correspondence filtering, while Stage 2 aggregates the intermediate restorations into a provisional pose-free 3D Gaussian representation whose input-view re-renders provide dense global guidance for final restoration. The resulting views enable a more coherent 3D representation and higher-quality novel-view synthesis. Experiments on real-world and synthetic Deblur-NeRF scenes show consistent gains over strong baselines, improving PSNR by 1.19 dB and 2.11 dB, respectively. Progressive ablations, cross-view correspondence visualization, and camera reprojection analysis further demonstrate improvements in both rendering quality and multi-view geometric consistency.

</details>


### [22] [Towards Unified Dynamic Face Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10346)
*Sebastian Regalado,Varshanth R. Rao,Ruowei Jiang,Parham Aarabi,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Unified Dynamic FLD方法，通过Face Part-Anchored Landmark Positions (FPALPs)概念将不同N点基准数据集统一化，使得单一模型可跨数据集训练并可在运行时输出任意数量的特定地标预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部地标检测方法有两个功能性局限：(1) 不同N点基准数据集需要独立训练不同的网络参数；(2) 在N点数据集上训练的模型只能可靠地输出这N个地标，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 首先提出FPALPs概念，将每个地标表示为沿面部部位轮廓从零（起点）到一（终点）的进度值，使任意来源数据集的地标都能以统一格式表达，从而将所有N点数据集合并为单一数据集。其次，用基于FPALP的查询表示每个地标，通过跨模态解码器逐步细化，并基于最终表示预测坐标。该方法称为Unified Dynamic FLD。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在保持竞争力的同时，在多个案例中超越了现有最先进方法，并实现了单模型多数据集学习与运行时灵活地标预测的优势。

Conclusion: Unified Dynamic FLD简化了地标检测流程，实现单模型支持任意数量N点数据集训练，并能通过加载指定地标查询在运行时输出任意数量的地标预测，同时保持甚至超越现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Although advancements in face landmark detection (FLD) methods continue to push performance boundaries, they overlook two major functional limitations: (1) different network parameters need to be trained independently for each ``$N$-point'' benchmark dataset, and (2) a model trained on an ``$N$-point'' dataset reliably outputs only the $N$ landmarks. In our work, we first conceptualize Face Part-Anchored Landmark Positions (FPALPs), wherein each landmark is treated as a progression value between zero (start) and one (end) along a face part's contour. Every landmark can be expressed in the FPALP format, irrespective of its source dataset, hence unlocking the ability to unify all ``$N$-point'' datasets into a single dataset. Secondly, we represent each landmark with an FPALP-based query, refine it progressively with a cross-modality decoder, and predict its coordinates based on the final representation. Our approach, called Unified Dynamic FLD, embodies these two design choices and streamlines the landmark detection pipeline by enabling (1) a single model to learn on any number of ``$N$-point'' datasets, and (2) yield any number of specific landmark predictions by loading the designated landmark queries at runtime. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that our method delivers these benefits while remaining competitive with, and in several cases outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [23] [FormStruct-Bench:A Hierarchical and Diagnostic Benchmark for Table-Form Document Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10396)
*Lujie Ban,Jiangtao Zhu,Yuanheng Yu,Jiasheng Shi,Chenhao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FormStruct-Bench，一个分层诊断基准，用于评估表格类文档的结构识别能力。通过70个可复用模板和7000个验证实例（其中1100个测试实例经人工审核），在页面、模式、组件三个层级上评估14个系统，发现文档级得分最高83.85%，但细粒度结构得分低于18%，揭示内容阅读与层级结构恢复之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么评估整体文档输出，要么评估传统表格网格，其聚合分数无法揭示结构失败的具体位置。需要一种分层的诊断性基准，能够将聚合性能追溯到具体的结构失败模式，以便可靠地推进表格形式文档的结构识别研究。

Method: 构建70个可复用模板，通过保留来源的Director-Artist-Verifier流水线扩展为7000个验证实例；测试集中1100个实例额外经过人工审核以确保可审计性。评估协议包含5个主要指标和3个结构特异性诊断，覆盖页面、模式和组件层级，并提供难度、结构约束和视觉退化三个切片维度。

Result: 在14个API托管/本地部署系统及2个SFT变体上，最佳文档级得分为83.85%，而最佳细粒度结构得分低于18%。文档级得分显著高于细粒度结构得分，说明现有系统在阅读内容方面表现较好，但在恢复层级和区域组织方面存在明显短板。

Conclusion: 当前系统在阅读表格文档内容上已取得较好成绩，但在恢复支撑可靠表格理解所需的层级结构和区域组织方面仍存在巨大鸿沟。FormStruct-Bench提供了一种能将聚合性能追溯到具体结构失败模式的分层诊断工具，为表格结构识别研究提供了更精确的评估框架。

Abstract: Transforming table-form documents into machine-processable records requires recovering not only their visible content but also the multilevel structure that organizes it. However, existing benchmarks evaluate either holistic document outputs or conventional table grids, and their aggregate scores provide little insight into where structural failures occur. We introduce FormStruct-Bench, a hierarchical and diagnostic benchmark that evaluates table-form document structure recognition at both the document level and progressively finer component levels, allowing aggregate performance to be traced back to specific structural failure modes. To construct auditable ground truth at scale, we annotate 70 reusable templates and expand them into 7,000 verified instances through a provenance-preserving Director--Artist--Verifier pipeline; all 1,100 instances in the template-disjoint test set additionally receive human review. Our evaluation protocol uses five primary metrics and three structure-specific diagnostics across page, schema, and component levels, together with slices over difficulty, structural constraints, and visual degradation. Across 14 API-hosted and locally deployable systems plus two SFT variants, the best document-level score reaches 83.85%, whereas the best reported fine-grained structural score remains below 18%. These results reveal a pronounced gap between reading document content and recovering the hierarchy and regional organization required for reliable table-form understanding.

</details>


### [24] [A second-order theory of texture for depth from focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10411)
*Sreekar Ranganathan,Ioannis Gkioulekas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于波动光学的粗糙表面光学纹理外观理论，揭示即使是传统计算机视觉认为无纹理的表面，也会因表面微几何产生主观散斑（二阶纹理），并证明通过窄带光谱滤波器可增强该纹理对比度，从而显著改善自然环境光下无纹理场景的被动深度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动深度从对焦（depth from focus）依赖于表面的纹理信息，但传统计算机视觉认为许多真实表面（如光滑或均匀的表面）是无纹理的，这限制了深度重建的性能。作者希望揭示表面微观几何在波光学层面产生的纹理现象，并利用它来改善被动深度重建。

Method: 作者从波动光学出发，建立粗糙表面纹理外观的理论模型，解释了主观散斑（subjective speckle）的产生机制及其作为二阶纹理的特性。他们分析了这类纹理在自然环境光照下的对比度特性，并提出仅使用窄带光谱滤波器来增强散斑对比度，从而提升深度从对焦算法的输入信号质量。

Result: 通过理论分析、仿真和真实世界实验，作者验证了所提方法能够在看似无纹理的场景中大幅提升被动深度重建的效果，证明了窄带滤波增强主观散斑对比度的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作弥合了波动光学与计算机视觉之间的鸿沟，表明"无纹理"表面在微观尺度上并非真正无纹理，并提供了一个简单实用的光学手段（窄带滤波）来解锁此类二阶纹理，从而显著提升被动深度估计的能力。

Abstract: We present a theory of textured appearance of optically rough surfaces based on wave optics, emphasizing the role of texture for passive depth from focus. Our theory shows that even surfaces that traditional computer vision would consider textureless can produce textured appearance, due to subjective speckle from surface microgeometry. We analyze the properties of this second-order texture, and show that we can enhance its contrast under natural ambient lighting by simply using a narrowband spectral filter. Doing so results in dramatic improvements in passive depth reconstruction of seemingly textureless scenes, as we demonstrate through extensive theory, simulations, and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [25] [DriveVLA-M0: Failure-Aware Memory Augmentation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10413)
*Zebin Xing,Yupeng Zheng,Qiang Chen,Linbo Wang,Yichen Zhang,Pengxuan Yang,Junli Wang,Deheng Qian,Xiaoqing Ye,Junyu Han,Yifeng Pan,Qichao Zhang,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveVLA-M0，一种具有失败感知潜在记忆的检索增强VLA模型，用于端到端自动驾驶。通过构建失败案例记忆池并采用解耦LoRA的测试时训练机制进行检索和修正，在NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA驾驶模型缺乏利用历史失败经验或适应分布偏移的能力，导致在相似场景中反复犯错、持续表现不佳。作者旨在通过显式记忆和检索机制来解决这一局限性。

Method: DriveVLA-M0包含三个核心组件：(1) 潜在记忆池，存储失败案例及其结构场景表示和专家轨迹标签；(2) 专门的检索模型，解耦静态道路结构与动态智能体交互，实现结构化的场景检索；(3) 基于解耦LoRA的测试时训练（TTT）机制，在推理时将检索到的案例注入模型，实现针对性和场景特定的修正，且不修改主干网络。

Result: 在NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上，DriveVLA-M0在Navtest上达到94.1 PDMS，在Navhard上达到47.0 EPDMS，仅引入26.44ms的TTT反向传播延迟开销，并且随着记忆库扩展可实现无训练的性能增益。

Conclusion: DriveVLA-M0通过失败感知的记忆机制和高效的测试时训练，实现了对历史失败场景的针对性修正和分布偏移的适应，且开销极小、可扩展性强，为VLA模型在自动驾驶中利用历史经验提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving by enabling unified reasoning across perception, language, and planning. However, existing approaches lack mechanisms to exploit past failures or adapt to distribution shifts, causing the model to persistently underperform on similar scenarios where it has previously failed. In this paper, we propose DriveVLA-M0, a retrieval-augmented VLA with failure-aware latent memory. We construct a latent memory pool that stores failure cases along with their structure scene representations and expert trajectory labels, and design a dedicated Retrieve Model that decouples static road structure and dynamic agent interactions to enable structurally grounded retrieval. At inference time, retrieved cases are injected into the model via a lightweight decoupled LoRA-based test-time training (TTT) mechanism, allowing targeted and scenario-specific correction without modifying the backbone. Extensive experiments on NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmark demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms prior methods, achieving 94.1 PDMS on Navtest and 47.0 EPDMS on Navhard with only 26.44 ms TTT backward latency overhead. Furthermore, we show that DriveVLA-M0 scales effectively with additional memory, enabling training-free performance gains through memory expansion. The code is available at https://github.com/ZebinX/DriveVLA-M0.

</details>


### [26] [GeoSeg-OV: Bridging Geospatial Gaps with Structural Guidance for Open-Vocabulary Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10426)
*Ruizhong Liu,Tingzhang Luo,Zaiyan Zhang,Jundong Chen,Hongruixuan Chen,Shaoguang Huang,Hongyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoSeg-OV，一种开放词汇遥感分割方法，将辅助视觉基础模型（VFM）的特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦，重新用作结构引导用于代价聚合和解码，在全局HRLC基准上超过SOTA +2.5/~2.7平均mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇遥感分割面临地理空间域偏移（异构区域、空间分辨率、采集平台差异）导致视觉-文本匹配弱化、跨数据集泛化受限的问题。现有方法引入辅助视觉基础模型（VFM）时，通常简单地将其特征与文本嵌入耦合作为额外匹配证据，但这可能引入不一致的匹配信号，且VFM结构敏感的表征未被充分利用。

Method: GeoSeg-OV将辅助VFM特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦，重定位为结构引导。具体包括：(1) 从多旋转CLIP特征构建方向鲁棒的代价体；(2) 冻结的VFM并行提取多尺度结构敏感特征；(3) 结构引导聚合（SGA）整合代价token、CLIP语义引导与VFM导出的成对结构偏差，实现连贯空间传播；(4) 代价感知解码（CAD）基于当前解码器上下文自适应细化并融合多尺度语义与结构引导。

Result: 在覆盖六大洲七个数据集的全局高分辨率土地覆盖（HRLC）基准上，GeoSeg-OV在两种训练设置下分别超越SOTA +2.5和+2.7平均mIoU。大规模零样本案例研究进一步验证了其在无需目标域标注或重训练的情况下跨地理域和类别系统的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将VFM特征从匹配信号中解耦并用作结构引导，比直接用于匹配更有效，能够增强跨域地理泛化能力和开放词汇分割性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary remote sensing segmentation has recently emerged as a promising paradigm that enables pixel-level recognition of arbitrary categories specified by natural language, including classes unseen during training. However, geospatial domain shifts caused by heterogeneous regions, spatial resolutions, and acquisition platforms weaken visual-text matching and limit cross-dataset generalization. Recent attempts have begun to incorporate auxiliary vision foundation models (VFMs), typically coupling their features with text embeddings as additional matching evidence. However, this strategy may introduce inconsistent matching signals while leaving the structure-sensitive representations of VFMs insufficiently exploited. We therefore propose GeoSeg-OV, which decouples auxiliary VFM features from visual-text matching and repurposes them as structural guidance for cost aggregation and decoding. GeoSeg-OV constructs an orientation-robust cost volume from multi-rotation CLIP features, while a frozen VFM extracts multi-scale structure-sensitive features in parallel. We propose Structure-Guided Aggregation (SGA), which integrates cost tokens and CLIP semantic guidance with VFM-derived pairwise structural biases for coherent spatial propagation, followed by text-conditioned class-wise reasoning. We further introduce Cost-Aware Decoding (CAD) to adaptively refine and fuse multi-scale semantic and structural guidance based on the current decoder context. On the global High-Resolution Land Cover (HRLC) benchmark spanning seven datasets across six continents, GeoSeg-OV outperforms the state-of-the-art by +2.5 and +2.7 average mIoU under two training settings. A large-scale zero-shot case study further demonstrates its generalization across geographic domains and category systems without target-domain annotations or retraining.

</details>


### [27] [Lesion-Aware Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator for CT-to-PSMA PET Synthesis in Prostate Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10429)
*Rashmi Bhaskara,Waleed M. Almutairi,Matthew Gopaulchan,Maram Musaad Alqurashi,Francis Asamoah,Alex Ocana,Clinton D. Bahler,Oluwaseyi M. Oderinde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出LAFNO，一种病灶感知的自适应傅里叶神经算子，用于CT到PSMA-PET的合成。它用两个CT衍生代理通道（对比度代理和紊乱代理）替代高维放射组学条件化，在瓶颈处注入模型，并结合全容积重建与病灶级监督（总病灶活性、肿瘤核心对比度、瘤周监督）。在TCIA数据集上，LAFNO保持竞争性的图像质量（SSIM 0.960/0.938），同时显著降低了TLA误差（48.3%/64.0%），并实现了最高的肿瘤核心放射组学可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习PET合成模型使用全局损失（如L1或MSE），对所有体素同等对待，导致全身PSMA-PET中占据体积很小的肿瘤体素信号被低估。直接使用放射组学条件化需要从勾画的病灶区域提取特征，耗时且复杂。因此需要一种高效、病灶感知的合成方法，既能保持全局图像质量，又能保留肿瘤特异的活性信号和结构。

Method: LAFNO是病灶感知的自适应傅里叶神经算子。核心创新包括：（1）用两个CT衍生代理通道——对比度代理（表征局部密度变化）和紊乱代理（表征局部纹理异质性）——替代高维放射组学条件化，注入模型瓶颈；（2）结合全容积重建与病灶级监督，包括总病灶活性（TLA）、肿瘤核心对比度和瘤周监督；（3）整个框架基于傅里叶神经算子架构，实现高效的自适应特征学习。

Result: 在TCIA PSMA-PET-CT-Lesions数据集上与四个基线架构对比：LAFNO在18F-和68Ga-PSMA上分别达到SSIM 0.960和0.938，保持整体图像质量竞争力；每患者TLA误差降至48.3%和64.0%；两种示踪剂的所有特征类别中实现了最高的肿瘤核心放射组学可重复性。但瘤周可重复性仍依赖于示踪剂类型，合成PSMA-PET的生物保真度仍具挑战。

Conclusion: LAFNO通过CT衍生的代理通道和病灶级监督，在保持整体图像质量的同时显著改善了病灶活性估计和肿瘤核心的放射组学可重复性。该方法为PET合成提供了从全局一致到病灶感知的范式转变，但瘤周区域的生物保真度仍需进一步改进，特别是针对不同示踪剂的合成。

Abstract: Deep learning models that synthesize PET from CT or MRI can reduce patient dose and scanner demand, but are typically optimized with global losses such as L1 or mean squared error (MSE) that treat all voxels similarly. In whole-body PSMA-PET, tumor voxels occupy only a small fraction of the volume, yet carry the clinically relevant activity signal; as a result, models can achieve high structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) while still underestimating lesion activity or failing to preserve tumor-specific structure. Radiomics provides biologically meaningful descriptors of tumor intensity and texture, but direct radiomics conditioning is time-consuming because it requires feature extraction from delineated lesion regions. We propose LAFNO, a Lesion-Aware Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator for CT-to-PSMA-PET synthesis that replaces high-dimensional radiomics conditioning with two efficient CT-derived proxy channels. Motivated by radiomics analysis of PSMA-avid tumor core and peritumoral regions, LAFNO uses a contrast proxy for local density variation and a disorder proxy for local texture heterogeneity, both injected into the model bottleneck. LAFNO combines whole-volume reconstruction with lesion-level total lesion activity (TLA), tumor-core contrast, and peritumoral supervision. We evaluated LAFNO against four baseline architectures on the TCIA PSMA-PET-CT-Lesions dataset. LAFNO remained competitive on whole-volume image quality, achieving SSIM of 0.960 and 0.938 for 18F- and 68Ga-PSMA, respectively, while reducing per-patient TLA error to 48.3% and 64.0% for 18F- and 68Ga-PSMA, respectively, and achieving the highest tumor-core radiomics reproducibility across all feature classes for both tracers. Peritumoral reproducibility remained tracer-dependent, indicating that biological fidelity in synthetic PSMA-PET remains challenging.

</details>


### [28] [DynaPPI: A Large-scale Dynamic Protein Dataset for AI-driven Advances in Protein Interactomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10435)
*Jiabao Wei,Zilong Geng,Yuze Wang,Jianjun Li,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Bing Zhang,Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaPPI，一个包含蛋白质复合物形成动态轨迹（从解离链到结合态）的分子动力学数据集，用于弥合静态结构生物学与动态分子相互作用之间的鸿沟，使扩散模型能够学习动态结合轨迹并预测未知多链蛋白复合物的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在蛋白质骨架生成中表现强大，但预测未知多链蛋白复合物（complex）的结构仍是未解决的挑战。根本原因在于现有静态或动态蛋白质数据集只关注静态快照或单一实体轨迹，忽略了多个单体形成复合物的动态过程。

Method: 构建DynaPPI动态蛋白质数据集，包含蛋白质复合物形成过程中从解离链到结合状态的分子动力学（MD）轨迹，使扩散模型能够显式学习已知复合物的动态结合轨迹。

Result: 借助该数据集，扩散模型可以准确预测未知复合物的结构，利用其多样的生成特性，从而推动AI驱动的结构生物学和蛋白质相互作用组学研究。

Conclusion: DynaPPI作为连接静态结构生物学与动态分子相互作用时序性质的桥梁性资源，可进一步催化AI驱动的结构生物学和蛋白质相互作用组学（protein interactomics）的发展。

Abstract: Diffusion models have been widely explored in protein backbone generation due to their powerful generation capabilities.However, in today's AI-driven biological research, predicting the structure of unknown multi-chain protein aggregates (called "complexes" in biology) remains an unsolved challenge.This is because existing static or dynamic protein datasets focus solely on static snapshots or single-entity trajectories, neglecting the dynamic process of multiple monomers forming complexes.To alleviate this dilemma, we present DynaPPI, a dynamic protein dataset comprising molecular dynamics (MD) trajectories of protein complex formation from dissociated chains to the bound state, as a pivotal resource to bridge the gap between static structural biology and the inherently temporal nature of dynamic molecular interactions.Benefiting from this dataset, diffusion models can explicitly learn the dynamic binding trajectories of known complexes and accurately predict the structures of unknown complexes based on their diverse generative properties, thereby further catalyzing AI-driven structural biology and protein interactomics.

</details>


### [29] [MammoMix: Leveraging Mixture of Experts for Robust Mammogram Breast Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10437)
*Dinh Tan Nguyen,Hoang Quan Dang,Chen Zhang,Sai Ho Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MammoMix框架，基于混合专家（MoE）范式实现乳腺钼靶病变检测的鲁棒性和泛化性。每个专家模型针对特定领域训练，通过门控机制自适应加权融合专家输出，并引入MoCAE校准模块调整置信度以反映真实预测不确定性。在CSAW、DDSM、DMID三个公开数据集上的实验表明，MammoMix在平均精度和可靠性上均优于基线检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺钼靶病变检测面临图像质量、病变外观和人群统计特征在不同数据集间的差异性挑战。现有目标检测器（如YOLO和DETR）在单一数据集上表现良好，但在异构源上训练或应用时性能显著下降。亟需一种能够跨领域泛化的鲁棒检测框架。

Method: MammoMix采用混合专家（MoE）范式：1）多个专家模型分别在不同领域数据上训练，实现各专家对特定域特征的专门化；2）引入门控机制，根据输入图像自适应地加权各专家贡献，实现域自适应推理；3）集成MoCAE校准模块，调整置信度分数以反映真实预测不确定性，提升可靠性。

Result: 在CSAW、DDSM和DMID三个覆盖不同临床场景的公开乳腺钼靶数据集上，MammoMix在平均精度和可靠性两方面均优于基线检测器，尤其在数据变异性更大的数据集上优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 专家专门化与校准后的集成融合能够显著增强模型泛化能力和鲁棒性。MammoMix为跨真实临床领域的可靠AI辅助乳腺癌筛查提供了一个有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Breast lesion detection in mammography remains a challenging task due to variations in image quality, lesion appearance, and population demographics across datasets. While current object detectors such as YOLO and DETR achieve strong results on individual datasets, their performance often degrades when trained on or applied across heterogeneous sources. To address this, we propose MammoMix, a novel framework based on Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm for robust and generalizable lesion detection. In MammoMix, each expert model is trained on a specific domain, allowing it to specialize in distinct characteristics of its source data. A gating mechanism adaptively weighs contributions from each expert based on input image, combining their outputs to enable domain-adaptive inference. To improve reliability, we further incorporate a calibration module, MoCAE, which adjusts confidence scores to reflect true predictive uncertainty. We evaluate MammoMix on 3 public mammography datasets: CSAW, DDSM, and DMID, covering diverse clinical settings. Results show that MammoMix outperforms baseline detectors in both average precision and reliability, particularly on datasets with greater variability. Our findings demonstrate that expert specialization and calibrated ensemble fusion significantly enhance model generalization and robustness. MammoMix offers a promising step toward dependable AI-assisted breast cancer screening across real-world clinical domains.

</details>


### [30] [Stream Forcing: Constructing Unified Training Trajectory for Robust Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10439)
*Yueting Zhu,Yuehao Song,Kaicheng Zhang,Bao Tang,Shaoyu Chen,Qian Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Stream Forcing，一个统一的流式视频生成训练框架，通过将视频扩散采样重公式化为帧索引的随机过程，构造从独立采样到推理一致采样的连续训练轨迹，配合联合校准和时序相关采样算法，解决流式视频扩散模型中训练-推理不一致与训练覆盖不足的矛盾。在UCF-101基准上实现36.6%的FVD改进，并支持零样本长时视频外推（27.9% FVD改进）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成在世界建模中具有重要潜力，但流式视频扩散模型存在根本性的训练-推理不匹配问题：推理阶段遵循特定的去噪顺序，而先进的训练策略通常需要多样化的噪声水平配置。这种不一致导致难以同时兼顾训练充分性和推理一致性。

Method: 将视频扩散采样重公式化为帧索引的随机过程（frame-indexed stochastic process）；在该随机过程空间中构造一条连续训练轨迹，使采样调度逐步从独立采样演进到推理一致采样；引入联合校准算法（joint calibration）保证轨迹平滑性，引入时序相关采样算法（temporal correlative sampling）增强跨帧相关性。

Result: 在UCF-101基准上，Stream Forcing实现36.6%的FVD改进（生成质量）；在零样本长时视频生成外推场景下实现27.9%的FVD改进，展示了对长时间跨度视频生成的鲁棒外推能力。

Conclusion: Stream Forcing作为一个统一的流式视频生成训练框架，在训练充分性和推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，显著提升了流式视频生成质量，并支持稳健的零样本长时视频外推。

Abstract: Streaming video generation holds strong potential for world modeling, where future frames must be inferred online sequentially to form a continuous video stream. However, streaming video diffusion models introduce a fundamental train-inference mismatch: inference follows a specialized denoising order, whereas advanced training strategies typically require diverse noise-level configurations. To address this trade-off between train-inference consistency and training coverage, we reformulate the video diffusion sampling as a frame-indexed stochastic process over noise levels. Within this stochastic process space, we construct a continuous training trajectory along which the sampling schedule progressively evolves from independent sampling to inference-consistent sampling. We further introduce a joint calibration algorithm and a temporal correlative sampling algorithm to ensure trajectory smoothness and cross-frame correlation. Building on these designs, we propose Stream Forcing, a unified training framework for streaming video generation that balances training sufficiency and inference efficiency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Stream Forcing significantly improves generation quality with a 36.6% FVD improvement on the UCF-101 benchmark. Furthermore, our method facilitates robust zero-shot extrapolation to long-horizon video generation with a 27.9% FVD improvement on the UCF-101 benchmark.

</details>


### [31] [FUSE: Frame-Unified Stress Estimation from Facial Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10442)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Thomas Kassiotis,Yang Guo,Guangliang Li,Giorgos Giannakakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FUSE（Frame-Unified Stress Estimation）框架，将完整面部视频作为一个整体输入进行压力检测，无需时间窗口切分或外部分割。通过将时间维度折叠到空间表示的通道维度，并采用统一的不对称注意力架构处理高维输入。在58名受试者的压力数据集上，FUSE在时间步长t=15时达到最高测试准确率69.44%，全帧配置达到69.03%，证明了在该场景下时间窗口化并非必需。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的压力检测方法普遍将完整录音分解为短时间窗口后再进行分类，这引入了窗口长度、重叠和聚合等额外选择，同时也限制了跨整个录音的时间信息直接分析。作者希望消除时间窗口化这一中间步骤，实现对整个录音的端到端统一建模。

Method: FUSE将完整录音的所有帧融合为一个统一的二维表示，具体通过将时间维度折叠到空间表示的通道维度来实现。得到的的高维输入由一个统一的非对称注意力架构处理。实验评估了7种时间步长配置（从全帧输入到稀疏子采样），采用分层受试者级别的协议。

Result: 在58名受试者的压力数据集上，FUSE在t=15时达到最高测试准确率69.44%，全帧配置（t=1）为69.03%。计算成本在12.48至348.78 GFLOPs之间变化，展示了时间密度与效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在该设置下，时间窗口化并非面部视频压力检测的必要条件，完整录音推理可以在单一统一架构内实现。

Abstract: Automatic stress detection from facial video offers a practical path to non-intrusive affect monitoring, yet existing video-based approaches commonly decompose full recordings into short temporal windows before classification. This design introduces additional choices regarding window length, overlap, and aggregation, while limiting direct analysis of temporal information across the entire recording. In this study, we present FUSE (Frame-Unified Stress Estimation), a facial-video stress detection framework that processes complete recordings as a single input without temporal windowing or external segmentation. The name reflects the defining operation of the method: rather than dividing a recording into short clips, all frames are fused into one unified two-dimensional representation from which the stress state is estimated. This unification is realized by folding the temporal dimension into the channel dimension of the spatial representation, and the resulting high-dimensional input is processed using a unified asymmetric-attention architecture. At a temporal stride of t = 1, FUSE retains the full 120-second recording as one input, corresponding to 3,600 frames at 30 fps. Experiments on a 58-subject stress dataset using a stratified subject-level protocol evaluate seven temporal-stride configurations, ranging from full-frame input to sparse subsampling. FUSE achieves the highest test accuracy of 69.44% at t = 15, while the full-frame configuration remains competitive at 69.03%. Across the stride range, computational cost varies from 12.48 to 348.78 GFLOPs, showing the trade-off between temporal density and efficiency. These results demonstrate that temporal windowing is not required for effective facial-video stress detection in this setting, and that complete-recording inference can be achieved within a single unified architecture.

</details>


### [32] [Bridging Event Streams and DiT: Event-Guided Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10479)
*Guixu Lin,Yuyang Yu,Xiang Ji,Linyao Chen,Zhengwei Yin,Mengshun Hu,Mingdeng Cao,Shengfeng He,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于适配器的框架，将事件相机的高时间分辨率运动线索注入预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，用于视频帧插值，无需从头训练事件辅助模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型在视频帧插值中虽能合成中间帧，但面对大时间间隔和复杂运动时会出现运动模糊、结构失真和时间不一致等问题。事件相机能提供高时间分辨率的运动线索以弥补这些不足，但现有方案需从头训练事件辅助模型，成本高昂。

Method: 提出适配器（adapter）框架，将事件线索注入预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，仅需极小的架构改动。具体利用图像扭曲事件（Image Warped Events, IWEs）和双向稀疏光流，在生成过程中提供空间和时间对齐的结构与运动引导。

Result: 在真实和合成基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在减少插值伪影、提升重建保真度与时间一致性方面，一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过适配器方式引入事件引导线索，可在不大幅改动预训练模型的前提下显著提升扩散模型视频帧插值的质量，为事件辅助插值提供了一种高效且有效的范式。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models have recently advanced video frame interpolation by synthesizing intermediate frames between input images. However, handling large temporal gaps and complex motion remains challenging, often resulting in motion blur, structural distortions, and temporal inconsistencies. Event cameras provide high-temporal-resolution motion cues that are well suited for bridging these gaps and improving interpolation quality. To exploit this advantage without training an event-assisted model from scratch, we propose an adapter-based framework that incorporates event-derived cues into a pre-trained image-to-video diffusion model with minimal architectural changes. Specifically, our method leverages Image Warped Events (IWEs) and bidirectional sparse optical flow to provide spatially and temporally aligned guidance during generation. By injecting these event-guided structural and motion cues into the diffusion process, our approach reduces interpolation artifacts and improves both reconstruction fidelity and temporal coherence. Experimental results on real and synthetic benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is at https://joseph-lin-tech.github.io/BridgeEventDiT-VFI/.

</details>


### [33] [When Vision Becomes Text: Visual Token Pruning via Cross-Modal Residual Guidance in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10489)
*Congyang Ou,Ruike Song,Yang Zhou,Libo Sun,Haokui Zhang,Zhenbo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE，一种无需训练的视觉token压缩方法，通过Cross Modal Residual (CMR)量化文本子空间无法解释的视觉信息，结合文本注意力相关性和残差空间多样性保留任务相关及互补的token，在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅保留11.1%的视觉token即可保持97.5%的平均性能，实现3.62倍prefill加速和6.02倍KV缓存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法依赖基于相似性的引导，仅捕获局部层级信号而忽略VLM整个推理过程，缺乏对文本token在深层LLM中逐步吸收视觉信息这一全局现象的量化与利用。

Method: 1) 提出Cross Modal Absorption (CMA)从几何表征视角量化视觉信息被文本吸收的程度；2) 提出Cross Modal Residual (CMR)，通过Tikhonov正则化最小二乘将视觉token投影到文本子空间并利用重建残差量化无法被文本解释的视觉信息；3) 提出SIEVE，无需训练，综合CMR、文本注意力相关性和残差空间多样性进行视觉token压缩。

Result: 在多种VLM架构上验证了SIEVE的有效性。在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上，仅保留11.1%的视觉token即可保持97.5%的原始平均性能，实现3.62倍prefill加速、2.49倍端到端加速以及6.02倍的KV缓存缩减。

Conclusion: 通过揭示LLM深层中文本token持续吸收视觉信息的现象，并利用残差视角将其与相似性引导互补，SIEVE实现了高效且任务友好的视觉token压缩，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低推理成本。

Abstract: Abundant visual information strengthens vision-language model (VLM) perception, yet massive visual tokens raise inference costs. Existing visual token pruning methods rely on similarity-based guidance, which exploits pairwise text-vision and vision-vision token correlations for compression. However, such methods only capture local layer-level signals and overlook the whole inference process in VLM. In this paper, we revisit VLM inference and present a new efficient guidance scheme that complements similarity-based guidance. In particular, we identify a key observation: as LLM layers deepen, text tokens continuously aggregate visual information via self-attention and progressively absorb partial visual content into textual representations. To quantify this phenomenon, we propose Cross Modal Absorption (CMA) from a geometric representation perspective to measure how much visual information is absorbed by text, revealing that more visual tokens in deeper layers can be approximately explained by the text subspace. We accordingly propose Cross Modal Residual (CMR). It projects visual tokens onto the text subspace via Tikhonov regularized least squares and exploits reconstruction residuals to quantify visual information that cannot be explained by text. Finally, based on CMR, we present SIEVE, a training-free visual token compression method that combines CMR, text-attention relevance, and residual-space diversity to retain task-relevant and complementary tokens. Experiments on diverse VLM architectures verify the effectiveness of SIEVE. For instance, on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, SIEVE keeps only $11.1\%$ of visual tokens while preserving $97.5\%$ of the original average performance, achieving $3.62\times$ prefill speedup, $2.49\times$ end-to-end speedup, and a $6.02\times$ KV-cache reduction.

</details>


### [34] [SapiensID 2.0: Aligning Human Recognition Foundation Models with Human Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10497)
*Yiyang Su,Jie Zhu,Feng Liu,Anil K. Jain,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SapiensID 2.0是一个融合语义与时间感知的人类识别框架，通过从多模态大语言模型中迁移零样本语义知识到判别性嵌入空间，并设计运动学语义注意力头来捕捉时间动态特征，在行人重识别、步态识别和面部识别任务上均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在人类识别任务上主要依赖静态几何特征提取，这与人类感知方式根本不同。当前模型存在"语义盲区"问题，容易过拟合瞬时噪声而无法利用不变的软生物特征，同时难以捕捉时间维度的运动特征。这种对静态、瞬时信息的过度依赖导致识别性能受限。

Method: 1) 利用不变特征对齐(ITA)解决多模态大语言模型语义空间与判别性嵌入空间之间的维度不匹配问题，蒸馏核心持久特征；2) 设计瞬态噪声解缠(TND)模块分离衣物等干扰伪影；3) 提出运动学语义注意力头(K-SAH)，将空间注意力扩展到时间窗口，通过跟踪语义patch随时间变化来捕捉丰富的运动学特征，无需大规模视频数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明SapiensID 2.0在基于图像和视频的行人重识别、步态识别任务上均达到最先进性能，同时保持鲁棒的面部识别能力。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态大语言模型的零样本语义知识与时间运动学建模相结合，SapiensID 2.0有效弥合了现有模型与人类感知之间的鸿沟，证明了语义与时间感知联合建模对人类识别任务的关键价值。

Abstract: While foundation models have significantly advanced human recognition across diverse modalities, they predominantly rely on static, geometric feature extraction.
