Motivation: 基于LLM的搜索代理在回答问题时需要通过多步交互与外部环境协作，但将完整执行轨迹提供给LLM会导致上下文无限增长并引入噪声。现有压缩方法在减少上下文时丢失重要细节，且常替换错误事实却不修复由其推导出的下游推理结果。

Method: ReTree构建了一个有界的每步推理上下文，同时保留源链接的证据。它将搜索建模为一棵证据树，节点存储有界摘要、证据和修订历史。当新检索到的证据与早期声明矛盾时，ReTree回溯到引入该声明的节点，替换过时证据、重新生成摘要、剪除受影响分支并恢复搜索。基于源的证据溯源支持可靠的冲突定位，使最终声明可追溯至检索到的段落。

Result: 在四个公开的问答与搜索基准上，ReTree持续优于Full-Trajectory ReAct，将答案准确率提升最高25.6个百分点；Full-Trajectory ReAct的平均每步最大推理上下文是ReTree的1.27-1.51倍。

Conclusion: ReTree被确立为长时程搜索中一种有效的自校正记忆抽象，在保持推理上下文有界的同时实现了精确的事实修正和下游推理修复。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based search agents answer questions through multi-step interactions with external environments. However, providing complete execution trajectories to the LLM causes unbounded context growth and introduces noise. Existing compression methods reduce context at the cost of important details and often replace erroneous facts without repairing downstream reasoning derived from them. To address this problem, we propose ReTree, a self-correcting tree-structured memory mechanism for search agents. ReTree constructs a bounded per-step reasoning context while preserving source-linked evidence. It models search as an evidence tree whose nodes store bounded summaries, evidence, and revision histories. When newly retrieved evidence contradicts an earlier claim, ReTree traces back to the node where the claim was introduced, replaces outdated evidence, regenerates summaries, prunes affected branches, and resumes search. Source-grounded evidence provenance supports reliable conflict localization and keeps final claims traceable to retrieved passages. Experiments on four public question-answering and search benchmarks show that ReTree consistently outperforms Full-Trajectory ReAct, improving answer accuracy by up to 25.6 percentage points (pp); the average maximum per-step reasoning context of Full-Trajectory ReAct is $1.27$--$1.51\times$ that of ReTree. These results establish ReTree as an effective self-correcting memory abstraction for long-horizon search.

</details>


### [236] [TRIBE: Predicting Team Performance via Communication Behavior Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06926)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali,Nikolos Gurney,David V. Pynadath,Fred Morstatter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIBE，一种领域无关的方法，通过分析团队通信模式来揭示传统性能指标无法捕捉的团队行为动态，能够在任务开始10%时即可将团队归类为可预测绩效的行为部落，并在四个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计能有效协助人类团队的自主智能体需要理解团队动态，但传统性能指标往往无法揭示隐藏的团队行为模式，且缺乏任务特定知识，因此需要一种领域无关的方法来捕捉和分析团队行为动态。

Method: TRIBE采用通信模式分析，将团队分类为可预测绩效的行为部落；通过时间分析揭示AI智能体与人类顾问对团队行为轨迹的不同影响；并与Llama进行比较以优化流水线，实现显著加速和性能提升。

Result: 在四个多样化数据集上验证了通信模式对团队绩效的预测能力，预测强度随任务结构允许的行为自由度而变化；发现AI智能体会显著改变团队行为轨迹，而人类顾问与自然动态一致；团队在整个协作过程中保持行为灵活性；优化后的流水线实现了显著加速并带来性能提升。

Conclusion: TRIBE通过领域无关的通信模式分析，为团队行为动态提供了早期可预测的洞察，支持及时干预，并揭示了AI智能体与人类顾问对团队协作的不同影响模式。

Abstract: Designing autonomous agents that effectively assist human teams hinges on understanding team dynamics, often without task specific knowledge. We present TRIBE, a domain independent approach that reveals team behavioral dynamics invisible to traditional performance metrics. We show that communication patterns can categorize teams into performance predictive behavioral tribes, as early as 10% into the task, enabling timely interventions. We test TRIBE on four diverse datasets and demonstrate that communication patterns predict team performance while the prediction strength varies by the degree a task structure allows for behavioral freedom. Our temporal analysis reveals that AI agents significantly alter team behavioral trajectories while human advisors align with natural dynamics, and that teams maintain behavioral flexibility throughout collaboration. Further, we compare TRIBE to Llama and optimize the pipeline, achieving significant speedup with performance improvement.

</details>


### [237] [Ex-Omni-2D: Expressive Omni-Modal Dialogue Models with Native Visual Presence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10720)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zhipeng Li,Xiaoying Tang,Tianshu Yu,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ex-Omni-2D，一个全模态对话框架，能够生成协调的文本、个性化语音和基于参考条件的视频响应。通过视觉思维计划（VTP）和共享声学-时间接口，实现了异质数据下的高效训练，并通过流式学生网络实现了实时推理（端到端RTF 1.293）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态对话模型虽能理解多模态输入并合成语音回复，但其响应在视觉上是"脱离身体"的（visually disembodied），即缺乏与语音同步的虚拟形象动画，无法提供真正身临其境的对话体验。

Method: Ex-Omni-2D分两步：(1) 给定多模态查询、参考图像和参考音频，模型预测结构化视觉思维计划（描述场景、情感和动作），生成回复文本和原生多码本语音单元；(2) 语音单元解码为语音并与视频帧在线对齐，形成共享声学-时间接口，使回复路径和虚拟形象路径可分别从异质数据（语音、对话、虚拟形象视频）中学习，无需大规模查询-文本-语音-视频联合监督。全序列视频生成器作为教师模型，蒸馏为少步块因果流式学生模型，其前缀流式机制在连续块间传递干净隐状态以减少累积劣化。

Result: 在四步推理下，完整的四GPU流水线在400×720/720×400分辨率下实现了1.293的端到端实时因子（RTF），在质量与效率之间提供了实用的操作点。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni-2D通过解耦语音与视频生成并通过共享接口对齐，以及教师-学生的知识蒸馏策略，在高效率下实现了协调的多模态对话响应生成，为全模态对话系统提供了实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Omni-modal dialogue models can understand multimodal inputs and synthesize spoken replies, yet their responses remain visually disembodied. We introduce \textbf{Ex-Omni-2D}, an omni-modal dialogue framework that generates a coordinated response comprising text, personalized speech, and reference-conditioned video. Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


### [238] [Tree-of-Ideas: Automated Research Ideation via Cross-Trajectory Reasoning over Scholarly Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10740)
*Xun Li,Yiying Yang,Pengtao Li,Xiao Yao,Suyu Liu,Xiaoyang Ye,Ziyu Lu,Yuan Yao,Yangning Li,Yinghui Li,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)两阶段框架，通过EvoTrace从引用关系中重建分支化的学术演化轨迹，再由EvoAgent跨轨迹推理生成有依据的研究想法，在六个AI研究主题上取得最优自动方法得分(6.27/10)，接近人类论文参考水平(6.29)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究方法要么将论文视为无结构上下文，要么将学术演化建模为孤立的引用链，忽略了研究轨迹之间的交互作用。有效的科研构思需要超越对先前工作的静态理解，追踪研究问题和解决方案在文献中的演化过程。

Method: 提出两阶段框架ToI：(1) EvoTrace从引用关系中重建分支化的学术轨迹，追踪演化中的方法、已解决的问题和存在的空白；(2) EvoAgent跨轨迹进行推理，识别汇聚的问题和互补的解决方案，从而生成有依据的研究想法。

Result: 在六个AI研究主题上，ToI在自动方法中取得最高分(6.27 vs 最强基线5.36，满分10分)，其中新颖性(Novelty)得分6.36、依据性(Groundedness)得分7.00，且整体得分接近人类论文参考(6.29)。

Conclusion: 跨路径的演化推理对于生成有效研究想法具有重要价值，ToI通过结合轨迹重建与跨轨迹推理，能够产生接近人类水平的研究构思。

Abstract: Effective research ideation requires moving beyond a static understanding of prior work to trace how research problems and solutions evolve across the literature. Existing methods either treat papers as unstructured context or model scholarly evolution as isolated citation chains, overlooking interactions among research trajectories. We propose Tree-of-Ideas (ToI), a two-stage framework. EvoTrace reconstructs branching scholarly trajectories from citations, tracking evolving methods, resolved problems, and gaps. EvoAgent then reasons across trajectories to identify convergent problems and complementary solutions, generating grounded research ideas. Across six AI research topics, ToI achieves the highest score among automatic methods (6.27 vs. 5.36 for the strongest baseline on a 10-point scale), with strong Novelty (6.36) and Groundedness (7.00). Also, its score approaches that of human-paper references (6.29), demonstrating the value of cross-path evolutionary reasoning.

</details>


### [239] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准生成与评估框架，从扁平单标签动作语料合成四级层次化意图基准（动作/活动/低层意图/高层意图），保留真实动作级特征。通过转移模型和覆盖感知采样构建情节，并设计生成规则与评估逻辑规则分离以规避循环监督风险。在15,002个情节上，四个基线均表现出0.13-0.17的compositional held-out gap，表明该差距是基准的结构属性而非模型伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语料存在两难：大规模语料提供原子化单标签片段但缺乏时序组合与语义层次；复合活动语料虽有层次但领域狭窄、层级固定。需要一种能从扁平动作语料合成多层次、长时间跨度意图基准的方法，同时规避合成数据带来的循环监督风险。

Method: (1) 从扁平单标签动作语料合成四级层次（动作、活动、低层意图LLI、高层意图HLI）基准，保留动作级真实预提取特征；(2) 通过转移模型（transition model）在主体一致性约束下组装情节；(3) 使用覆盖感知采样器将主体使用基尼系数从0.566降至0.248；(4) 设计上将序列生成规则与评估所用一阶逻辑规则保持不相交，从设计层面规避循环监督；(5) 采用逻辑无关基线（logic-free baseline）验证违背语义规则的速率，以及乱序控制（order-destroying control）进行生成器一致性检验。

Result: 框架实例化为15,002个情节。四个不同模型家族的代表性基线在所有模型上均出现0.13-0.17 macro-F1的compositional held-out gap，即使表现最好的图感知模型也未能闭合该差距；逻辑无关基线违背held-out语义规则的速率高于其内在数据率；乱序控制产生的macro-F1变化处于种子波动范围内。

Conclusion: 该compositional held-out gap是基准的结构属性而非模型伪影，说明现有模型在层级化组合推理上存在系统性短板。基准的ontology、转移模型和生成器均已公开，支持再生成与扩展，为层次化行为理解研究提供了一个可复现、可验证的评估底座。

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [240] [Rule of Thumb: Explaining Artificial Intelligence Systems using Partial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10766)
*Kaivalya Rawal,Daria Onitiu,Brent Mittelstadt,Sandra Wachter,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"经验法则"（RoT）解释，一种基于新颖公式的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法，通过识别对AI系统行为预测最相关的特征来提供逐数据点解释，适用于零样本分类、黑盒AI审计和科学发现等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法在解释AI系统决策时存在局限，需要一种既能满足监管要求、又具备模型无关性、且计算效率更高、并提供友好可视化界面的新解释方法。

Method: 提出"Rule of Thumb"（RoT）解释方法，其核心是采用一种公式化的方式来识别用于预测AI系统行为的最相关特征（针对特定数据点），从而生成简洁、可理解的经验法则式解释。

Result: RoT方法在大语言模型的零样本分类、无需模型访问权限的黑盒AI审计以及AI辅助科学发现三个场景中展现出良好适用性；同时满足主流AI法规的具体要求，提供熟悉的界面与可视化，具备模型无关性，且计算速度大幅优于现有替代方案。

Conclusion: RoT是一种新颖且多用途的XAI方法，兼具法规合规性、模型无关性、高效率与易用性，为可解释人工智能提供了新的实用途径。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to explain how an Artificial Intelligence (AI) system arrived at a particular decision. We propose ''Rule of Thumb'' (RoT) explanations, a new approach to XAI based upon a novel formulation that identifies the most relevant features for predicting the behaviour of an AI system, for a particular datapoint. We show how RoT is well-suited to enable XAI in: (a) zero-shot classification using large language models (LLMs), (b) auditing of opaque AI systems without model access, and (c) the use of AI in scientific discovery. Additionally, RoT meets specific requirements from leading AI regulations, provides a familiar interface and visualisations for XAI practitioners, is model-agnostic, and is substantially faster than alternatives.
  Code available at: https://github.com/KaiRawal/Rule-of-Thumb-Explaining-Artificial-Intelligence-Systems-using-Partial-Information

</details>


### [241] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu,Ligang Huang,Zeli Su,Zewei Pan,Zhaoyang Han,Xing Chen,Yuanfeng Song,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉技能卡（VSC）作为一种状态条件记忆表征，将可复用的GUI操作流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定。基于此构建SkillLens系统，通过Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card从异构交互经验构建VSC，推理时检索相关卡片并选择性展开证据供冻结的视觉语言模型执行器进行GUI动作预测；同时CardDistill利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文，训练无需运行时检索的学生模型。在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym基准上，SkillLens分别提升冻结GPT-5.4-mini执行器+11.6步长成功率与+2.9整体分数，CardDistill进一步提升Qwen3-VL-2B学生模型+12.0和+3.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体虽能感知丰富的软件界面，但其决策缺乏视觉程序记忆：智能体可能识别单个控件，却无法识别当前正在进行的熟悉工作流、下一步该操作哪个控件，或什么证据能确认进展。原始交互轨迹虽保留此类信息但过长且有噪声，而纯文本技能策略往往遗漏决定程序适用性的视觉状态。

Method: 提出视觉技能卡（VSC）这一状态条件记忆表征，将可复用程序与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定。SkillLens通过Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card从异构交互经验构建VSC，推理时检索相关卡片并仅选择性扩展所需的证据，供固定视觉语言模型执行器生成接地GUI动作预测。此外CardDistill利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文，训练无运行时卡片检索的学生模型。

Result: 在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym上，SkillLens使冻结GPT-5.4-mini执行器分别提升+11.6点Step SR和+2.9点Overall；CardDistill使仅学生模型的Qwen3-VL-2B指标分别提升+12.0和+3.2点。

Conclusion: 视觉技能卡作为状态条件记忆表征能有效弥合GUI智能体决策中视觉程序记忆的缺失；SkillLens与CardDistill两种范式分别验证了检索增强推理和知识蒸馏在利用视觉技能卡提升GUI动作预测方面的有效性。

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [242] [ChemWorld: Programmable Chemical Worlds for Controlled and Replayable Agent Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10792)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChemWorld是一个可编程化学环境系统，将可复用的过程和观测组件编译为可执行世界，分离公开实验契约与隐藏的化学/材料法则，支持世界组成和条件的系统变化、单一私法干预的隔离测试、完整轨迹的精确回放与审计，为自主化学研究提供可控且可复现的实验基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主化学研究需要代理能够反复行动、观察和适应的环境。物理实验室提供真实材料证据但成本高且难以进行紧密匹配的干预实验，而大多数数字环境将底层实验世界保持固定不变，难以系统地测试不同实验条件下的行为差异。因此需要一种可编程、可干预、可回放的环境来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: ChemWorld采用编译方法将可复用的过程组件和观测组件构造为可执行世界。其核心架构特点包括：（1）将公开实验契约（agent可见）与评估者拥有的化学和材料法则分离；（2）支持变化世界组成和操作条件，或在保持公开任务和交互条件不变的情况下改变单一隐藏法则；（3）通过事务性执行记录操作、失败、资源变化和状态转换，实现完整的环境-行动轨迹的精确回放和审计；（4）通过父-子世界分叉对来隔离单一私法干预的效果。

Result: 全面普查资格测试覆盖了参考注册表、52个生成组成及模块/接口/编译/无效操作测试。8个确定性实验案例展示了共享生命周期语义、故障恢复和精确回放；6个父子世界分叉对在匹配的公开条件下隔离了单一私法干预的效果。一个独立agent还通过同一公开接口在非参考世界中完成了完整生命周期。

Conclusion: 在声明的组件和模型域内，ChemWorld为跨系统变化的化学世界的实验研究提供了可控且可回放的基底，作为物理实验室证据和校准的补充。

Abstract: Autonomous chemistry increasingly depends on environments in which agents can repeatedly act, observe, and adapt.Physical laboratories provide essential real-material evidence but are costly to repeat and difficult to use for tightly matched interventions, whereas most digital environments keep the underlying experimental world largely fixed. We introduce ChemWorld, a programmable chemical environment in which reusable process and observation components are compiled into executable worlds. ChemWorld separates the public experimental contract available to an agent from evaluator-owned chemical and material laws. Researchers can therefore vary world composition and operating conditions, or change a single hidden law while holding the public task and interaction conditions fixed. Transactional execution records operations, failures, resource changes, and state transitions, allowing complete environment-action trajectories to be replayed exactly and audited. Full-census qualification covered the reference registry, 52 generated compositions, and module, interface, compilation, and invalid-action tests. Eight deterministic experimental cases demonstrated shared lifecycle semantics, failure recovery, and exact replay, while six parent-child world-fork pairs isolated the effects of single private-law interventions under matched public conditions. An independent agent also completed a full lifecycle in a non-reference world through the same public interface. Within the declared component and model domain, ChemWorld provides a controlled and replayable substrate for studying experimentation across systematically varied chemical worlds, complementary to physical-laboratory evidence and calibration.

</details>


### [243] [EvoMem: Memory-Augmented Evolution for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10795)
*Viktor Volkov,Valentin Khrulkov,Andrey V. Galichin,Danil Sivtsov,Nikita Glazkov,Olga Volkova,Konstantin Pchelin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry V. Dylov,Petr Anokhin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoMem是一种为基于LLM的进化程序搜索设计的持久记忆架构，能够捕获并重用候选变异知识，通过两阶段机制（运行后提取存储、后续进化中检索指导变异）实现跨运行和跨任务的策略复用，在多个基准测试中展现出正向改进并减少了冗余探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动进化框架在每次运行后都会丢弃成功的变异策略知识，导致重复发现相似想法，限制了跨运行和跨任务学习的能力。研究者希望通过持久记忆架构来捕获和复用这些可迁移的知识。

Method: EvoMem包含两个阶段：(1) 每次进化运行结束后，将成功的变异事件转化为结构化、任务感知的建议并附带来源信息进行存储；(2) 在后续进化过程中，根据当前任务和程序上下文检索一小批相关指令来引导变异操作。

Result: 在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU内核优化及相关基准测试中，EvoMem在大多数评估设置下取得了目标指标或搜索速度的正向平均改进，同时也揭示了不同任务间的性能变异性。

Conclusion: EvoMem证明了持久记忆可以减轻部分冗余探索，并改善LLM驱动进化搜索中成功策略的复用与适应能力，为进化搜索中的跨运行和跨任务知识传递提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Successful mutation strategies in evolutionary code search may contain reusable knowledge that is useful beyond a single run, and in some cases may transfer across related tasks and domains. However, existing LLM-driven evolutionary frameworks largely discard such knowledge, repeatedly rediscovering similar ideas and limiting opportunities for cross-run and cross-task learning. We introduce EvoMem, a persistent memory architecture for LLM-based evolutionary program search that captures and reuses candidate mutation knowledge. EvoMem converts successful mutation events into structured, task-aware advice for future runs. It operates in two phases: after each run, it extracts and stores promising ideas with provenance, and during subsequent evolution, it retrieves a small set of relevant instructions based on the current task and program context to guide mutation. Across geometric optimization, multi-hop question answering, GPU kernel optimization, and related benchmarks, our experiments show positive average improvements in target metrics or search speed for most evaluated settings, while also revealing variability across tasks. Overall, EvoMem provides evidence that persistent memory can reduce some redundant exploration and improve the reuse and adaptation of successful strategies in LLM-driven evolutionary search.

</details>


### [244] [Hypothesis Frontier: Verifier Guided LLM and Symbolic Search for First-Order Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10843)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hypothesis Frontier，一个验证者引导的神经符号框架，用于一阶概念合成问题。该框架在每一轮中评估LLM生成的每个公式在所有训练对象上的表现，保留最强的验证假设，并利用剩余错误指导后续生成。符号处理修复无效公式并简化训练验证公式，最终在相同的模型、问题集和LLM轮数预算下，比重复原始提示生成解决更多问题，并能压缩许多训练验证公式而不改变任何训练预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶概念合成需要在多个有限关系结构上推断能一致分类标记对象的公式。虽然每个候选可以精确评估，但量化一阶公式构成巨大的搜索空间，且LLM输出往往语义上可行但不完全正确。需要一种方法将LLM的生成能力与精确符号验证相结合，以提高问题解决率并压缩公式。

Method: 提出Hypothesis Frontier框架：1) 在每个训练对象上精确评估LLM生成的每个公式；2) 在各轮次中保留最强的已验证假设作为前沿；3) 利用剩余错误反馈指导后续LLM生成；4) 符号处理修复无效公式，同时锚定在LLM生成的假设上；5) 对训练验证通过的公式进行精确简化，保持所有训练预测不变。

Result: 在匹配的模型、问题集和LLM轮数预算下，Hypothesis Frontier比重复原始提示生成解决了显著更多的问题。最终公式选定后，精确简化在保持每个训练预测不变的前提下，缩短了许多训练验证公式。

Conclusion: 精确符号推理既有助于解决更多归纳问题，也能压缩许多生成的公式，验证了验证者引导与符号处理在LLM驱动的一阶概念合成中的有效性。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [245] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了利用欧几里得几何信息的新颖约束过滤算法，在约束逻辑编程（CLP）中实现，以增强旅行商问题（TSP）及其欧几里得广义变体（EGTSP）的约束传播强度，并通过实验证明了计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束编程（CP）文献中，欧几里得TSP通常被当作一般TSP处理——即计算完整的距离矩阵而忽略节点坐标所携带的几何信息。这导致了约束传播强度的不足，无法充分利用几何结构来剪枝搜索空间。

Method: 提出在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架中实现的新过滤算法，利用节点的平面坐标和欧几里得度量来挖掘几何信息，实现比现有方法更强的约束传播。该方法还可扩展到欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）等其他欧几里得TSP变体。

Result: 实验结果展示了所提出方法相对于现有方法在计算效率上的显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过利用坐标几何信息进行更强的约束传播，可以有效提升欧几里得TSP及其变体（如EGTSP）在约束编程框架中的求解性能，该方法在智能车辆和智能交通系统等工程应用中有实用价值。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [246] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（具身-数字融合智能体）范式，将数字智能体与具身智能体的能力统一起来，把"人的状态与能动性"作为建模、干预和评估的核心对象，构建感知-建模-预测-支持人的状态轨迹的闭环系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI存在结构性缺陷：数字智能体仅能转换软件状态，具身智能体仅能转换物理状态，二者都无法真正理解人的意图与状态（如老人漏服药物是被遗忘、困惑、副作用还是故意拒绝），也无法判断何种支持恰当。人的进化的状态和自主性未被作为建模、干预与评估的首要对象。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents范式，核心为四层闭环框架：1）基于事件的多模态感知重建有意义的个人事件；2）纵向可修正记忆提供时间上下文；3）个人世界模型（Personal World Models）预测在不同决策和干预下未来个人状态与结果；4）可采纳干预策略在知情同意、不确定性、安全性、可逆性和用户控制约束下选择相称的支持。人与环境反馈持续更新循环。采用有目的约束（purpose-bounded）、不确定性感知、用户可修正的表示而非穷尽的数字孪生。

Result: 统一了个人助手、健康智能体、AI陪伴和自适应人-AI系统中的碎片化能力，将软件工具、传感器、可穿戴设备、机器人和人类服务作为行动通道；组织设计空间（人态目标、关系情境、智能体角色）；提出以场景为中心的评估、自主性保持指标、基准需求、边缘原生个人模型及治理方向。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents将Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类福祉，主张以人的状态轨迹为核心、以相称支持为原则，在技术部署中维护人的自主性与控制权。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [247] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍IO Factory，一个用于模拟信息与影响力战役的AI驱动框架，将规划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量和适应等全过程整合为可追溯的仿真流程，支持多达10万代理的大规模运行，并生成可检查的证据以支持可复现的研究与红队分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵的威胁已从单个语言模型的具有说服力的文本扩展至AI蜂群（即持续存在的、协调一致的代理群体），这些代理能适应平台反馈并将有组织的战役伪装为普通社交互动。由于此类战役无法通过孤立的单一消息识别，必须在一个从规划到适应的连续谱系中进行分析，因此需要一个能够模拟和追踪这一完整过程的框架。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台内代表整个影响战役过程，连接行动者角色、平台行动、曝光记录、基于模型的结构化评估以及模拟人群中配置的变化。框架实现了完整的架构，并在多达10万代理的配置下进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，IO Factory能够在大规模条件下执行战役时间线，并产生可检查的曝光证据以及配置信念变量的测量移动。通过记录每次运行中的行动者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则，框架支持可复现的研究和红队分析。

Conclusion: IO Factory为模拟和检测AI协调影响力战役提供了可扩展、可追溯、可复现的研究平台，填补了传统上仅分析孤立消息文本的检测方法在处理AI蜂群式协调操纵方面的空白。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [248] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架，通过在每个推理步骤动态检索并注入已解决的示例到思维链中，为大语言推理模型提供推理指导，在多个基准上显著提升准确率，最高相对提升60%（AIME 2025）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言推理模型（LRMs）通过分配额外的推理时计算来生成扩展的思维链，但现有研究表明，顺序测试时扩展往往产生收益递减甚至负收益，因为更长的推理轨迹表现出更高的不确定性、错误累积和偏离原始问题的问题。

Method: ThinkRetrieve框架在推理过程的每个中间步骤，从外部语料库中检索相关的问题-逐步解答对，并将这些已解决的示例直接注入到推理轨迹中。与仅提供相关事实不同，该方法为模型提供如何推理的指导。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，针对GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ基准的实验表明，ThinkRetrieve相比标准测试时扩展一致地提升了准确率，在AIME 2025上获得高达60%的相对增益。

Conclusion: 动态检索已解决示例并将其注入推理轨迹，是一种有效的测试时扩展策略，能够缓解长链推理中的错误累积和问题漂移问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [249] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedCGR是一个联邦生成式跨域推荐框架，通过将物品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID（SID）序列，利用共享词汇表而非私有交互数据来实现跨域物品对齐，并通过可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤信号以及原型个性化生成器（按域相关性选择性聚合共享参数）来解决联邦场景下的领域异质性导致的负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域推荐（CDR）在联邦部署下面临核心矛盾：跨域对齐通常依赖重叠用户和共享交互信号等行为锚点，但联邦场景中这些锚点涉及隐私、稀疏甚至不可用。现有SID-based生成式方法直接联邦化会面临两个约束：(1) SID tokenizer需固定以保证跨客户端token一致性，导致仅依赖语义信号而无法全局共享或对齐本地CF信号；(2) 标准联邦平均在领域异质性下会导致负迁移。

Method: FedCGR包含两个核心设计：(1) 可靠性感知语义接口——将本地CF证据注入语义空间，弥补语义only的瓶颈；(2) 原型个性化生成器——根据域间相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时将域特定参数保持在本地，以实现显式适配。

Result: 在6个Amazon跨域场景下，FedCGR一致优于联邦生成式基线，并在全排序和采样评估协议下与强序列推荐及联邦CDR方法达到竞争性甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将联邦CDR重新建模为"在稳定语义物品语言上的生成"是可行的路径——共享词汇表而非共享交互数据即可实现跨域物品对齐，同时通过显式适配（原型个性化与可靠性感知注入）有效缓解联邦领域的异质性挑战。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [250] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出XCoT-VLA，用紧凑的可执行思维链令牌替代冗长的自然语言CoT，实现自动驾驶中高效、实时的推理与轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型依赖冗长的自然语言CoT进行推理，这种方式开放性过强、解码成本高、且难以作为面向动作的表示进行优化，不适合实时控制场景。

Method: 1) 从自动构建的Reason-Action监督中学习紧凑的可执行CoT令牌(XCoT)，轨迹数据提供动作证据，场景上下文提供因果语义；2) 预测的XCoT序列保持在上下文中，通过共享多模态自注意力条件化固定的轨迹查询；3) 采用确定性令牌-函数路由：Reason FFN处理XCoT令牌，Control FFN处理轨迹查询，用于流匹配轨迹生成；4) 进一步提出XCoT策略优化(XCPO)作为可选的精化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布集上纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323，换道场景横向FDE从1.616降至0.648。仅用2-6个可执行XCoT令牌表示驾驶推理，大幅降低自回归推理开销，保持在实时规划预算内。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向的推理可以做到紧凑、可执行，并直接与轨迹生成相关联，为VLA模型的实时自动驾驶控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [251] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FiLLM框架，通过可执行计算树从真实表格生成金融推理基准，答案通过符号评估保证正确性且无需人工标注。实验发现推理深度增加导致准确率下降高达51%，对抗性数值扰动下降高达47个百分点；轻量LoRA微调可将准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA上提升5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域已取得显著进展，但针对结构化数据的金融推理评估仍相对不足。作者希望构建一个可扩展、答案正确性有保障的金融推理基准，以填补这一空白。

Method: V-FiLLM从真实表格出发，基于可执行计算树生成推理基准。计算树通过符号评估获得ground truth，再渲染为自然语言问题，从而将模型排除在标注循环之外，实现无标注成本、任意规模生成，且不继承生成器的错误率。框架暴露四个独立可控的难度维度：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文规模。

Result: 在开源模型上实验发现：推理深度增加时准确率最多下降51%；在对抗性数值扰动下最多下降47个百分点。轻量LoRA微调（基于已验证的思维链轨迹）将保留问题准确率从81.1%提升到85.6%，并在FinQA上超越基础模型5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在表格金融推理中仍存在鲁棒性挑战，同时表明低成本的LoRA微调在有验证思维链轨迹的引导下，是提升组合推理能力的有效方向，为金融QA领域提供了可行的适应策略。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [252] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip是一种无需评估的技能压缩方法，通过寻找技能最短的忠实结构化解释（typed minimum description-length目标），在压缩自进化智能体的可复用技能时，同时保留触发条件、工作流、工具契约和稀有异常等关键信息，并提供一次性模式和持续Zip-on-Write模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体在积累技能时会出现重复冗余（同一需求被多次表述、常见动作序列被复制而非复用），导致技能注入成本高、难以维护。通用提示压缩不适用于技能场景，因为技能不是扁平文本，包含名称/描述（何时适用）、工作流（执行控制）、工具与输出契约（约束有效性）以及可能未被采样任务激活但依旧重要的稀有异常。评估引导的压缩引入rollout成本和评估集依赖，需要无评估的方法。

Method: SkillZip的核心直觉是"解释一次、多次引用"（explain once, reference many）：在适用作用域处只陈述一次重复规则，将重复动作序列分解为共享过程，只保留差异作为显式异常。形式化为带类型的极小描述长度（MDL）目标，定义在技能契约和残差之上，并对每个提取的触发条件、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段施加硬覆盖约束。该形式化提供简单共享阈值、按构造保留唯一稀有规则，并支持高效局部更新。提供两种模式：一次性模式（一次结构化提取调用+确定性优化）和持续Zip-on-Write模式（整合每个自进化补丁，无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史）。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化能力和开销成本方面均展现了有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip提出了一种无评估引导的按结构压缩技能的方法，能够在保持技能完整契约和稀有异常的前提下实现有效压缩，与自进化智能体的持续学习流程自然集成，兼具性能优势和低成本。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [253] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了城市级轨道交通站点知识图谱RTSKG数据集，显式建模不同类型城市实体间的空间与语义交互，并以Linked Data形式发布，实验验证其在站点周边店铺推荐与知识增强的客流预测任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测）需要大规模城市数据，但当前研究在数据组织上往往忽略了各类城市实体之间复杂的交互关系，缺乏对空间和语义交互的显式建模。

Method: 构建Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG)数据集，设计统一schema整合异构城市实体（轨道交通站点、道路段、兴趣点POI等），显式建模实体间的空间与语义交互，并以Linked Data形式在线发布。

Result: 在站点周边店铺推荐和知识增强的客流预测两个任务上的评估表明，RTSKG能够有效支持城市级轨道交通站点分析，验证了其有效性和实用潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG作为城市级轨道交通站点知识图谱数据集，通过统一schema显式建模异构城市实体的空间与语义交互，能够有效支撑站点相关下游任务，展现广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [254] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现agentic编码README（如CLAUDE.md）中的指令会无界增长，称之为"灾难性记忆"（catastrophic remembering），其根源在于不完美的回忆导致删除指令成本呈指数级增长。作者在1,867个仓库的247,694条指令生命周期中实证了该现象，并提出提示词注释（prompt comments）作为解决方案，可在合成基准上减少99.3%的多余指令，并在真实场景中将指令遵循性能提升高达23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实仓库中的agentic编码README（如CLAUDE.md）会无界增长，直到仓库退役或被整体重写。这一现象的根本原因是不完美的回忆：追加指令总是廉价，但一旦指令背后的理由丢失，删除它而不冒正确性回退风险的成本是O(2^|D|)。这形成了与持续学习中的"灾难性遗忘"相对的"灾难性记忆"——提示词系统化累积且无法清理的悖论。

Method: 首先，作者在1,867个仓库中对247,694条指令的生命周期进行大规模实证分析，量化了agentic提示词的无界增长。其次，通过"反转IFEval"构建可验证的世界（最优提示词已知），验证了注释编码潜在推理（latent reasoning）可以显著减少多余指令。最后，将同样的反转方法应用于WildIFEval，测试真实世界中提示词注释对agentic指令遵循的改进效果。

Result: 实证发现：agentic提示词在其生命周期中增长超过三倍（+226%），每个提交净增4.9条指令；指令越老，被删除的概率越低（log风险比-0.032/提交）。在合成基准上，编码潜在推理的注释消除了99.3%的多余指令（增长率从+211.3%降至+1.4%）。在WildIFEval上，提示词注释将真实agentic指令遵循性能提升高达23.1%。

Conclusion: 面向LLM的"提示词即代码"（prompt-as-code）范式同样需要像普通代码那样支持注释机制。正如"英语是新的编程语言"，注释作为代码维护的基本工具，也应当成为agentic提示词的标配，以遏制灾难性记忆带来的无界增长。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [255] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: sLTN是Logic Tensor Networks（LTN）的扩展，将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为语言中的一等公民，使结构组织能够被显式量化、关联并用于表达时间、序列和关系约束，同时保持与原始LTN语义的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架主要适用于扁平个体集合的数据表示，无法显式捕获时间顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构组织信息，限制了其在时序和关系推理任务中的应用。

Method: 提出sLTN框架：将结构维度（命名张量轴）引入为一等语言元素，支持对结构维度的显式量化、结构关系表达及逻辑层面的约束；形式化了sLTN的语法和模糊张量语义，证明无结构维度时退化为原始LTN语义；并基于PyTorch实现了包含声明式签名、公式解析和张量解释的实现。

Result: 形式化定义了sLTN的语法与模糊张量语义，证明了其与原始LTN语义的特例关系，提供了基于PyTorch的开源实现，并在典型的时间与序列推理示例上进行了展示。

Conclusion: sLTN通过将结构信息融入神经符号框架的逻辑层面，拓展了LTN在时空、序列和关系结构数据上的表达能力，为结构感知的神经符号推理提供了统一的框架基础。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [256] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过改进Grothendieck常数K_G的界限这一案例，深入探讨了AI在数学研究中的应用，展示了AI系统能够产生被领域专家认为新颖的见解，并讨论了其优势、劣势以及为AI创造突破性洞察的理想条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在数学研究中的使用日益增多，但如何有效使用它们尚不明确，需要通过具体案例研究来理解AI助力数学研究的实践方法与条件。

Method: 以一个广泛的案例研究为方法，系统记录了使用AI研究系统改进Grothendieck常数K_G上下界的过程，涉及将上界收紧至π/(2log(1+√2))-10^{-4}，下界收紧至6π/11，并详细分析了AI产生新颖见解的过程与条件。

Result: 成功的将K_G的最佳已知界限收紧为6π/11 ≤ K_G ≤ π/(2log(1+√2)) - 10^{-4}，且这些改进源于AI系统产出的、被领域专家认定为新颖的见解。

Conclusion: AI在数学研究中能够产生突破性见解并带来实质性的理论改进，但其有效运用需要创造理想条件，理解AI的优势与局限对于最大化其科研价值至关重要。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Amulet: Frame Extrapolation Through Sparse Layered Scene Representation and Adaptive Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10423)
*Sebastian Künzel,Fabian Schmierer,Sergej Geringer,Guido Reina,Daniel Weiskopf,Dieter Schmalstieg*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Amulet提出了一种基于稀疏、分块、分层中间场景缓存（cache）的高频帧外推渲染方法，通过显式光栅化并存储潜在可见几何体，预测性地填充未来视图的着色信息，从而实现无需神经网络的、能显式处理去遮挡区域的多帧外推，在4K分辨率下可达250Hz，性能上与DLSS等帧生成方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于重投影（reprojection）的帧生成方法在处理新出现的遮挡区域（disocclusion）时容易产生伪影或需要幻觉生成，且无法在高帧率下保持高精度着色。作者希望探索一种非神经网络的、能显式处理去遮挡的稀疏分层图像空间表示，以实现高质量的多帧外推。

Method: Amulet的核心是一种预测性填充分层图像空间缓存：1）将场景显式光栅化并存储到分块、分层的缓存中，缓存中预填未来视图所需的着色信息，并在多个当前帧中分摊计算成本；2）通过从前往后（front to back）的分层缓存遍历来合成新视图，并对过期或缺失的着色进行按需精化；3）采用基于梯度预测的调度器为每个tile分配生命周期，自适应地调整着色更新频率以应对运动和动态光照；4）将光栅化与着色率与显示刷新率解耦。

Result: Amulet能够用一个着色的帧合成多个外推帧，仅需少量局部更新。在典型应用中，可将60Hz的着色率外推至240Hz显示，4K分辨率下可达250Hz。在多个指标上与DLSS和神经光流（neural-flow）等最先进的帧生成方法竞争并达到相当水平，且能高质量外推比当代方法更多的帧。

Conclusion: Amulet探索了稀疏分层图像空间表示的设计空间，展示了一种精确、非神经、能显式处理去遮挡的多帧外推方法。其在多个场景中能以与延迟受限的帧插值方法相当的质量外推更多帧，为高刷新率渲染提供了一种新的可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce Amulet, a rendering method that transforms a scene into a sparse, tiled and layered intermediate scene representation (cache) for high-frequency frame extrapolation. In contrast to reprojection-based techniques, Amulet explicitly rasterizes and stores potentially visible geometry in its layered image-space cache, allowing accurate shading and inpainting of newly disoccluded regions without hallucination. Our key contribution is a cache that is predictively filled with shading information for future views, amortized over multiple current frames. Novel views are synthesized by hierarchically traversing the cache front to back and refining stale or missing shading on the fly. Using a predictive, gradient-based scheduler that assigns lifetimes for each tile, we enable adaptive shading updates under motion and dynamic lighting. Amulet decouples the rasterization and shading rate from the refresh rate of the display. In many scenarios, our cache can use a single shaded frame to synthesize multiple extrapolated frames with only a few localized updates. In a typical application, we extrapolate a 60 Hz shading rate to a 240 Hz display. Amulet achieves up to 250 Hz at 4K resolution and is competitive with state-of-the-art frame generation methods, including DLSS and neural-flow approaches, in multiple metrics. Amulet explores the design space of sparse layered image-space representation. It enables accurate, non-neural multi frame extrapolation with explicit handling of disocclusions. Our findings show that Amulet can extrapolate many more frames than contemporary methods with high quality, rivaling latency-bound frame interpolation methods with similar quality in many scenes.

</details>


### [258] [WildFireGS: Physics-Based Wildfire Simulation in Large-Scale Semantics-Enriched Gaussian Splatting Forest Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11100)
*Nienke Driessen,Joris Rijsdijk,Sören Pirk,Wojtek Palubicki,Dominik L. Michels,Michael Weinmann*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出WildFireGS，一个基于物理的野火模拟框架，直接在语义增强的大规模3D高斯溅射森林重建上运行，无需转换为显式网格或体素网格。该框架引入基于粒子的燃烧模型，能够在高斯表示上原生模拟点火、传热、燃烧和火焰传播，并通过降雨驱动的冷却机制实现模块化扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的野火模型虽然能高保真地模拟燃烧、传热和燃料动力学，但仅限于合成环境，需要完整且理想化的森林结构知识，无法直接应用于通过航空影像获取的真实世界环境。这阻碍了基于观测数据的真实世界野火数字孪生的构建。

Method: WildFireGS通过三个关键技术创新实现：1) 将语义和材料属性（植被类型、燃料特征）增强到3D高斯基元上，弥合学习式场景重建与环境模拟之间的鸿沟；2) 提出一种原生作用于高斯表示的基于粒子的燃烧模型，模拟点火、传热、燃烧和火焰传播；3) 通过能量汇（energy-sink）机制实现降雨驱动的冷却过程，用于模拟火灾遏制。

Result: 在合成场景和真实航空森林拍摄数据上的评估表明，WildFireGS能产生物理一致的野火行为，再现随植被密度、风速和地形坡度变化的特征传播动力学。此外，通过新颖的防火带（firebreak）实验和生物量损失估计对模型进行了验证。

Conclusion: WildFireGS为从观测数据直接构建真实世界野火数字孪生提供了可行路径，其模块化设计支持扩展多种环境机制（如降雨冷却），为野火风险缓解和景观管理提供了更实用的模拟工具。

Abstract: Climate-driven environmental change is driving an increase in both the frequency and severity of wildfire events, making accurate simulation and prediction critical for effective risk mitigation and landscape management. While recent physics-based wildfire models achieve high realism by explicitly simulating combustion, heat transfer, and fuel dynamics, they remain largely restricted to synthetic environments with complete and idealized knowledge of forest structure, limiting their applicability to real-world environments captured via aerial imagery. To provide a pathway toward real-world wildfire digital twins derived directly from observational data, we present WildFireGS, a physics-based wildfire simulation framework operating directly on large-scale, semantics-enriched 3D Gaussian Splatting forest reconstructions. Our approach bridges learning-based scene reconstruction and environmental simulation by augmenting Gaussian primitives with semantics and material properties that encode vegetation type and fuel characteristics. We introduce a particle-based combustion model that operates natively on Gaussian representations, simulating ignition, heat transfer, combustion, and flame propagation across complex forest structures. This enables direct physics-based simulation of fire behavior on reconstructed real-world environments, without requiring conversion to explicit meshes or volumetric grids. We demonstrate the modularity of WildFireGS through a rain-driven cooling mechanism in terms of an energy-sink process to realistically model fire containment. Evaluations on synthetic scenes and real aerial forest captures show physically consistent wildfire behavior, reproducing characteristic dynamics including propagation scaling with vegetation density, wind velocity, and terrain slope. In addition, we validate our model through novel firebreak experiments and biomass loss estimation.

</details>
